\displaystyle\;\leq\;\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{4}{b}$}}+\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{3 N (1+a)^{N-1} \, e^{\frac{4aN}{\kappa-1}} \, \log(T+1)}{(T+1)^{\frac{bN}{2}}}$}}\;.

Proof of
Lemma

C.3
.

By assumption,
bt^{-\beta}-at^{-\kappa}\leq 1
for all
1\leq t\leq T
. Since
0\leq 1-x\leq e^{-x}
for all
x\leq 1
, we have that for any
1\leq t_{1}\leq t_{2}\leq T
,

\displaystyle\mprod_{t=t_{1}}^{t_{2}}(1-bt^{-\beta}+at^{-\kappa})^{N}\;\leq\;\exp\bigg(-bN\msum_{t=t_{1}}^{t_{2}}t^{-\beta}+aN\msum_{t=t_{1}}^{t_{2}}t^{-\kappa}\bigg)\;.

(13)

Applying this to the first quantity of interest followed by noting that
a,b\geq 0
and using
Lemma

C.2
(ii), we obtain the first bound that

\displaystyle\mprod_{t=1}^{T}(1-bt^{-\beta}+at^{-\kappa})^{N}\;\leq

\displaystyle\;\exp\bigg(-bN\msum_{t=1}^{T}t^{-\beta}+aN\msum_{t=1}^{T}t^{-\kappa}\bigg)

\displaystyle\;\leq

\displaystyle\;\exp\big(-bN\,\varphi_{1-\beta}(T+1)+aN\,\varphi_{1-\kappa}(T+1)\big)\;.

For the second bound, we define

\displaystyle t_{0}\;\coloneqq\;\sup\Big\{t\leq T\;\Big|\;\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{b}{2}$}}\leq at^{-(\kappa-\beta)}\Big\}\;.

Then by noting that
1-bt^{-\beta}+at^{-\kappa}\geq 0
for all
1\leq t\leq T
again,we can bound the quantity of interest as

\displaystyle\msum_{t=1}^{T}t^{-\zeta}\,\Big(\msum_{j=1}^{N}(1-bt^{-\beta}+at^{-\kappa})^{j-1}\Big)\,\mprod_{s=t+1}^{T}(1-bs^{-\beta}+as^{-\kappa})^{N}

\displaystyle=\;\msum_{t=t_{0}+1}^{T}t^{-\zeta}\,\Big(\msum_{j=1}^{N}(1-bt^{-\beta}+at^{-\kappa})^{j-1}\Big)\,\mprod_{s=t+1}^{T}(1-bs^{-\beta}+as^{-\kappa})^{N}

\displaystyle\quad+\Big(\mprod_{s=t_{0}+1}^{T}(1-bs^{-\beta}+as^{-\kappa})\Big)

\displaystyle\hskip 20.00003pt\times\Big(\msum_{t=1}^{t_{0}}t^{-\zeta}\,\Big(\msum_{j=1}^{N}(1-bt^{-\beta}+at^{-\kappa})^{j-1}\Big)\,\mprod_{s=t+1}^{t_{0}}(1-bs^{-\beta}+as^{-\kappa})^{N}\Big)

\displaystyle\leq\;\msum_{t=t_{0}+1}^{T}t^{-\zeta}\,\Big(\msum_{j=1}^{N}\Big(1-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{b}{2}$}}\,t^{-\beta}\Big)^{j-1}\Big)\,\mprod_{s=t+1}^{T}\Big(1-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{b}{2}$}}\,s^{-\beta}\Big)^{N}

\displaystyle\quad+\Big(\mprod_{s=t_{0}+1}^{T}\Big(1-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{b}{2}$}}\,s^{-\beta}\Big)^{N}\Big)\Big(\msum_{t=1}^{t_{0}}t^{-\zeta}\,\Big(\msum_{j=1}^{N}(1+at^{-\kappa})^{j-1}\Big)\,\mprod_{s=t+1}^{t_{0}}(1+as^{-\kappa})^{N}\Big)

\displaystyle\leq\;N\times\underbrace{\msum_{t=t_{0}+1}^{T}t^{-\zeta}\,\mprod_{s=t+1}^{T}\Big(1-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{b}{2}$}}\,s^{-\beta}\Big)^{N}}_{\eqqcolon S_{1}}

\displaystyle\quad+N(1+a)^{N-1}\times\underbrace{\Big(\mprod_{s=t_{0}+1}^{T}\Big(1-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{b}{2}$}}\,s^{-\beta}\Big)^{N}\Big)}_{\eqqcolon S_{3}}\times\underbrace{\Big(\msum_{t=1}^{t_{0}}t^{-\zeta}\,\mprod_{s=t+1}^{t_{0}}(1+as^{-\kappa})^{N}\Big)}_{\eqqcolon S_{2}}\;.

In the last line, we have used that
0\leq 1-\frac{b}{2}t^{-\beta}\leq 1
for
t\geq t_{0}+1
and
1+at^{-\kappa}\leq 1+a
. To control the three quantities, we first note that by (
13
) , we have

\displaystyle S_{3}\;\leq

\displaystyle\;\exp\Big(-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{b N}{2}$}}\msum^{T}_{s=1}s^{-\beta}\Big)\exp\Big(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{b N}{2}$}}\msum^{t_{0}}_{s=1}s^{-\beta}\Big)

\displaystyle\;\overset{(a)}{\leq}

\displaystyle\;\exp\Big(-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{b N}{2}$}}\msum^{T}_{s=1}s^{-\beta}\Big)\exp\Big(aN\msum^{t_{0}}_{s=1}s^{-\kappa}\Big)

\displaystyle\;\overset{(b)}{\leq}

\displaystyle\;\exp\Big(-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{b N}{2}$}}\,\varphi_{1-\beta}(T+1)+2aN\,\varphi_{1-\kappa}(T+1)\Big)\;.

In
(a)
above, we have noted that
\frac{b}{2}\leq as^{-(\kappa-\beta)}
for
s\leq t_{0}
; in
(b)
, we have used
t_{0}\leq T
and
Lemma

C.2
(ii) with
a,b\geq 0
. In the special case
\beta=1<\kappa
, the above yields

\displaystyle S_{3}\;\leq

\displaystyle\;(T+1)^{-\frac{bN}{2}}\exp\Big(2aN\,\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1-(T+1)^{-(\kappa-1)}}{\kappa-1}$}}\Big)

\displaystyle\;\leq

\displaystyle\;(T+1)^{-\frac{bN}{2}}e^{\frac{2aN}{\kappa-1}}\;.

We now control
S_{2}
. By (
13
) again, we have

\displaystyle S_{2}\;\leq

\displaystyle\;\msum_{t=1}^{t_{0}}t^{-\zeta}\exp\Big(aN\msum_{s=t+1}^{t_{0}}s^{-\kappa}\Big)

\displaystyle\;\leq

\displaystyle\;\msum_{t=1}^{T}t^{-\zeta}\exp\Big(aN\msum_{s=t+1}^{T}s^{-\kappa}\Big)

\displaystyle\;\overset{(c)}{\leq}

\displaystyle\;\exp\big(2aN\varphi_{1-\kappa}(T+1)\big)\times\Big(\msum_{t=1}^{T}t^{-\zeta}\exp\big(-2aN\varphi_{1-\kappa}(t+1)\big)\Big)

\displaystyle\;\overset{(d)}{\leq}

\displaystyle\;3^{\zeta}\exp\big(2aN\varphi_{1-\kappa}(T+1)\big)\,\msum_{t=1}^{T}(t+2)^{-\zeta}\exp\big(-2aN\varphi_{1-\kappa}(t+1)\big)\;.

In
(c)
above, we have applied
Lemma

C.2
(ii); in
(d)
, we have noted that
\sup_{t\in\mathbbm{N}}(t+2)^{\beta}/t^{\beta}=3^{\beta}
. If
\kappa\neq 1
and
a>0
, we can apply
Lemma

C.2
(iv) to get that

\displaystyle S_{2}\;\leq

\displaystyle\;\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{3^{\zeta}}{2a N}$}}(T+2)^{\max\{\kappa-\zeta,0\}}\,\exp\big(2aN\varphi_{1-\kappa}(T+1)\big)

\displaystyle\;=

\displaystyle\;\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{Q_2}{N (1+a+c)^{N-1}}$}}\,\exp\big(2aN\varphi_{1-\kappa}(T+1)\big)\;.

If
\kappa=1
or
a=0
, the bound from
(c)
above reads

\displaystyle S_{2}\;\leq

\displaystyle\;\exp\big(2aN\varphi_{1-\kappa}(T+1)\big)\,\msum_{t=1}^{T}t^{-\zeta}(t+1)^{-2aN}

\displaystyle\;\leq

\displaystyle\;\exp\big(2aN\varphi_{1-\kappa}(T+1)\big)\,\msum_{t=1}^{T}t^{-\zeta-2aN}

\displaystyle\;\leq

\displaystyle\;2\,\varphi_{1-\zeta-2aN}(T+1)\,\exp\big(2aN\varphi_{1-\kappa}(T+1)\big)\;=\;\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{Q_2}{N (1+a)^{N-1}}$}}\,\exp\big(2aN\varphi_{1-\kappa}(T+1)\big)\;,

where we have used
Lemma

C.2
(ii) in the last line. Now consider the special case with
\zeta=1<\kappa
, the bound from
(d)
becomes

\displaystyle S_{2}\;\leq

\displaystyle\;3\exp\big(2aN\varphi_{1-\kappa}(T+1)\big)\Big(\msum_{t=1}^{T}(t+2)^{-1}\exp\big(-2aN\varphi_{1-\kappa}(t+1)\big)\Big)

\displaystyle\;\leq

\displaystyle\;3\exp\Big(2aN\,\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1-(T+1)^{-(\kappa-1)}}{\kappa-1}$}}\Big)\msum_{t=1}^{T}(t+2)^{-1}

\displaystyle\;\leq

\displaystyle\;3e^{\frac{2aN}{\kappa-1}}\log(T+1)\;.

We are left with controlling
S_{1}
, which follows from a similar strategy as controlling
S_{2}
:

\displaystyle S_{1}\;\overset{\eqref{eq:prod:to:exp}}{\leq}

\displaystyle\;\msum_{t=t_{0}+1}^{T}t^{-\zeta}\exp\Big(-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{b N }{2}$}}\msum_{s=t+1}^{T}s^{-\beta}\Big)

\displaystyle\;\leq

\displaystyle\;\msum_{t=1}^{T}t^{-\zeta}\exp\Big(-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{b N}{2}$}}\msum_{s=t+1}^{T}s^{-\beta}\Big)

\displaystyle\;\overset{(a)}{\leq}

\displaystyle\;\exp\Big(-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{b N }{2}$}}\,\varphi_{1-\beta}(T+1)\Big)\msum_{t=1}^{T}t^{-\zeta}\exp\Big(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{b N}{2}$}}\,\varphi_{1-\beta}(t+1)\Big)

(14)

\displaystyle\;\leq

\displaystyle\;4^{\zeta}\exp\Big(-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{b N}{2}$}}\,\varphi_{1-\beta}(T+1)\Big)\msum_{t=1}^{T}(t+3)^{-\zeta}\exp\Big(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{b N }{2}$}}\,\varphi_{1-\beta}(t+1)\Big)\;.

In
(a)
above, we used
Lemma

C.2
(ii). For
\beta\neq 1
and
b\neq 0
, we can apply
Lemma

C.2
(iv) with
\frac{b}{2}>0
to obtain

\displaystyle S_{1}\;\leq

\displaystyle\;4^{\zeta}\exp\Big(-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{b N }{2}$}}\,\varphi_{1-\beta}(T+1)\Big)\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{(T+3)^{\max\{\beta-\zeta, 0\}}}{b N /2}$}}\exp\Big(\,\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{b N}{2}$}}\varphi_{1-\beta}(T+3)\Big)

\displaystyle\;=

\displaystyle\;\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{2^{2\zeta+1} (T+3)^{\max\{\beta-\zeta, 0\}}}{b N}$}}\exp\Big(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{b N}{2(1-\beta)}$}}\Big((T+3)^{1-\beta}-(T+1)^{1-\beta}\Big)\Big)

\displaystyle\;\overset{(b)}{\leq}

\displaystyle\;\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{2^{2\zeta+1} (T+3)^{\max\{\beta-\zeta, 0\}}}{b N}$}}\exp\Big(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{b N}{2(1-\beta) (T+1)^\beta}$}}\Big)\;=\;\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{Q_1}{N}$}}\;.

In
(b)
, we have used
Lemma

C.2
(iii) with
1-\beta\leq 1
. Meanwhile, if
\beta=1
or
b=0
, we have

\displaystyle S_{1}\;\leq

\displaystyle\;\exp\Big(-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{b N }{2}$}}\,\varphi_{1-\beta}(T+1)\Big)\msum_{t=1}^{T}t^{-\zeta}(t+1)^{bN/2}

\displaystyle\;\leq

\displaystyle\;\exp\Big(-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{b}{2}$}}\,\varphi_{1-\beta}(T+1)\Big)\msum_{t=1}^{T}t^{-\zeta+bN/2}

\displaystyle\;\leq

\displaystyle\;2\varphi_{1-\zeta+bN/2}(T+1)\exp\Big(-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{bN}{2}$}}\,\varphi_{1-\beta}(T+1)\Big)\;=\;\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{Q_1}{N}$}}\;.

For the special case with
\zeta=\beta=1
, the bound in (
14
) becomes

\displaystyle S_{1}\;\leq

\displaystyle\;\exp\Big(-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{b N }{2}$}}\,\varphi_{0}(T+1)\Big)\msum_{t=1}^{T}t^{-1}\exp\Big(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{b N}{2}$}}\,\varphi_{0}(t+1)\Big)

\displaystyle\;=

\displaystyle\;(T+1)^{-\frac{bN}{2}}\msum_{t=1}^{T}t^{-1}(t+1)^{\frac{bN}{2}}

\displaystyle\;\leq

\displaystyle\;(T+1)^{-\frac{bN}{2}}\msum_{t=1}^{T}t^{-(1-\frac{bN}{2})}

\displaystyle\;\overset{(c)}{\leq}

\displaystyle\;2(T+1)^{-\frac{bN}{2}}\,\varphi_{\frac{bN}{2}}(T+1)\;=\;2(T+1)^{-\frac{bN}{2}}\,\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{(T+1)^{bN/2} - 1}{bN/2}$}}\;\leq\;\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{4}{bN}$}}\;.

In
(c)
, we have used
Lemma

C.2
(ii) for both the case
1-\frac{bN}{2}\leq 0
and
1-\frac{bN}{2}\geq 0
.

Combining the bounds for the general cases, we obtain the first desired inequality that

\displaystyle\msum_{t=1}^{T}t^{-\zeta}\,\Big(\msum_{j=1}^{N}(1-bt^{-\beta}+at^{-\kappa})^{j-1}\Big)\,\mprod_{s=t+1}^{T}(1-bs^{-\beta}+as^{-\kappa})^{N}

\displaystyle\;\leq\;Q_{1}+\exp\Big(-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{b}{2}$}}\,\varphi_{1-\beta}(T+1)+u\varphi_{1-\xi}(T+3)+4a\,\varphi_{1-\kappa}(T+1)\Big)\,Q_{2}\;.

For the special case
\zeta=\beta=1<\kappa,\gamma
, combining the earlier bounds gives

\displaystyle\msum_{t=1}^{T}t^{-\zeta}\,\Big(\msum_{j=1}^{N}(1-bt^{-\beta}+at^{-\kappa})^{j-1}\Big)\,\mprod_{s=t+1}^{T}(1

\displaystyle-bs^{-\beta}+as^{-\kappa})^{N}

\displaystyle\;\leq\;\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{4}{b}$}}+\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{3 N (1+a)^{N-1} \, e^{\frac{4aN}{\kappa-1}} \, \log(T+1)}{(T+1)^{\frac{bN}{2}}}$}}\;.

∎

C.2
Contraction and integrability results

The next result is a standard result in convex analysis,
needed to handle projections performed in Algorithms
1
and
2
.

Lemma C.4
.

Let
\Psi
a be convex subset of
\mathbb{R}^{p}
.
Let
{\psi}^{\star}\in\Psi
. Then, for all
\psi\in\mathbb{R}^{p}
, we have:

\displaystyle\left\|\mathrm{Proj}_{\Psi}(\psi)-{\psi}^{\star}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq\left\|\psi-{\psi}^{\star}\right\|

Proof.

We have:

\displaystyle\|\psi-{\psi}^{\star}\|^{2}

\displaystyle=\|\psi-\mathrm{Proj}_{\Psi}(\psi)+\mathrm{Proj}_{\Psi}(\psi)-{\psi}^{\star}\|^{2}

\displaystyle=\|\psi-\mathrm{Proj}_{\Psi}(\psi)\|^{2}+2\left\langle\psi-\mathrm{Proj}_{\Psi}(\psi),\mathrm{Proj}_{\Psi}(\psi)-{\psi}^{\star}\right\rangle+\|\mathrm{Proj}_{\Psi}(\psi)-{\psi}^{\star}\|^{2}

Since by
[
49
, Proposition 1.1.9]
, we have:

\displaystyle\left\langle\psi-\mathrm{Proj}_{\Psi}(\psi),\psi^{\prime}-\mathrm{Proj}_{\Psi}(\psi)\right\rangle\leq 0

for all
\psi^{\prime}\in\Psi
, we can use this inequality at
\psi^{\prime}={\psi}^{\star}\in\Psi
to obtain:

\displaystyle\left\|\psi-{\psi}^{\star}\right\|^{2}\geq\left\|\psi-\mathrm{Proj}_{\Psi}(\psi)\right\|^{2}

and the result follows by taking the square root.
∎

We now state two lemmas that guarantee an amount of integrability sufficient to our analysis.

Lemma C.5
.

Let
p,q\in\mathcal{P}(\mathcal{X})
such that
\frac{\mathrm{d}p}{\mathrm{d}q}
exists, and such that

\chi^{2}(p;q)<+\infty
. Assume that
f\in L^{2}(q)
Then
|\int f\mathrm{d}p|<+\infty
.

Proof.

By assumption,
f\in L^{2}(q)
. Moreover,
\chi^{2}(p,q)<+\infty
, and thus we have

\frac{\mathrm{d}p}{\mathrm{d}q}-1\in L^{2}(q)
. Thus, the inner product is finite, and

\displaystyle\Big|\mint f\Big(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\mathrm{d}p}{\mathrm{d}q}$}}-1\Big)\mathrm{d}q\Big|

\displaystyle=\Big|\mint f\mathrm{d}p-\int f\mathrm{d}q\Big|\coloneqq M<+\infty

\displaystyle\implies M-\left\lvert\mint f\mathrm{d}q\right\rvert

\displaystyle<\int f\mathrm{d}p<M+\left\lvert\mint f\mathrm{d}q\right\rvert

∎

Lemma C.6
.

For all
\psi\in\Psi
, for all
m\geq 1
, and for all
k\geq 1
, we have:

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\mathrm{P}^{m}_{\psi}\phi(X_{1})\right\|^{k}\right]<+\infty\,.

Proof.

By analycity of the log partition function
\psi\longmapsto\log Z(\psi)
, we have
\int\left\|\phi\right\|^{k}\mathrm{d}p_{\psi}<+\infty
for all
\psi\in\Psi
,
and thus, the function
x\longmapsto\left\|\phi\right\|^{k}(x)\in L^{2}(p_{\psi})
for all
\psi
.
Consequently,
P_{\psi}^{m}\|\phi\|^{k}\in L^{2}(p_{\psi})
.
We can apply Lemma
C.5
to
\mathrm{P}_{\psi}^{m}\left\|\phi\right\|^{k}
to obtain
\mathbb{E}\left[\mathrm{P}^{m}\|\phi(X_{1})\|^{k}\right]<+\infty

for all
k\geq 1
and for all
m\geq 0
.
As a by-product, we obtain
\mathrm{P}_{\psi}^{m}\left\|\phi\right\|^{k}\in L^{2}(p_{{\psi}^{\star}})
, and thus so
\big\|\mathrm{P}^{m}_{\psi}\phi\big\|^{k}
.
∎

The following lemma is used multiple time in our analysis.

Lemma C.7
.

Assume
A3
. Let
q
be a positive integer. Let
f\coloneqq(f_{1},\dots,f_{q})
such that
f_{k}\in\{\phi_{i}\}_{i=1}^{p}\cup\{\phi_{i}\phi_{j}\}_{i,j=1}^{p}
for
k\in[q]
. Then, for all
\psi\in\Psi
, we have

\displaystyle\left\|\mathbb{E}\left[\mathrm{P}^{m}_{\psi}(f-\mathbb{E}\big[f(X^{\psi})\big])(X_{1})\right]\right\|\leq\alpha^{m}C_{\chi}\left(\mathbb{E}\left[\big\|\big(f-\mathbb{E}\big[f(X^{\psi})\big]\big)(X^{\psi})\big\|^{2}\right]\right)^{1/2}\left\|\psi-{\psi}^{\star}\right\|

Proof.

Let us note first that

\displaystyle\|\mathbb{E}\mathrm{P}^{m}_{\psi}\left[\big(f-\mathbb{E}\big[f(X^{\psi})\big]\big)(X_{1})\right]\|^{2}

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{=}\msum\limits_{i=1}^{q}\left(\mint\!\mathrm{P}^{m}_{\psi}\big(f_{i}-\mathbb{E}\big[f_{i}(X^{\psi})\big]\big)(x)\left(p_{{\psi}^{\star}}(x)-p_{\psi}(x)\right)\text{d}x\right)^{2}

\displaystyle=\msum\limits_{i=1}^{q}\left(\mint\!\mathrm{P}^{m}_{\psi}\big(f_{i}-\mathbb{E}\big[f_{i}(X^{\psi})\big]\big)(x)\left(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{ \mathrm{d} p_{ {\psi}^{\star}} }{ \mathrm{d} p_{ \psi} }$}}(x)-1\right)p_{\psi}(x)\text{d}x\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\overset{(b)}{\leq}\left(\mint\left(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\mathrm{d}p_\psi^\star}{\mathrm{d}p_\psi}$}}(x)-1\right)^{2}p_{\psi}(x)\mathrm{d}x\right)\msum\limits_{i=1}^{q}\mint\!\mathrm{P}^{m}_{\psi}\big(f_{i}-\mathbb{E}\big[f_{i}(X^{\psi})\big]\big)(x)^{2}p_{\psi}(x)\text{d}x

\displaystyle\leq\chi^{2}(p_{\psi},p_{{\psi}^{\star}})\msum\limits_{i=1}^{q}\big\|\mathrm{P}^{m}_{\psi}(f_{i}-\mathbb{E}\big[f_{i}(X^{\psi})\big])\big\|_{L^{2}(p_{\psi})}

\displaystyle\overset{(c)}{\leq}\alpha^{2m}\chi^{2}(p_{\psi},p_{{\psi}^{\star}})\msum\limits_{i=1}^{q}\big\|f_{i}-\mathbb{E}\big[f_{i}(X^{\psi})\big]\big\|_{L^{2}(p_{\psi})(\mathbb{R}^{d})}

\displaystyle\overset{(d)}{\leq}\alpha^{2m}C_{\chi}^{2}\left\|\psi-{\psi}^{\star}\right\|^{2}\mathbb{E}\big[\big\|\big(f-\mathbb{E}\big[f(X^{\psi})\big]\big)(X^{\psi})\big\|^{2}\big]\;.

Here, we used the fact that
P^{m}_{\psi}
admits
p_{\psi}
as an invariant measure in
(a)

[
34
, Eq. (1.2.2)]
, the Cauchy-Schwarz inequality in
(b)

∎

C.3
Miscellaneous

Lemma C.8
.

Let
f:\Psi\rightarrow\mathbbm{R}^{p}
be a differentiable function in the interior of
\Psi\subseteq\mathbbm{R}^{p}
. For
\psi\in\Psi
, define
\sigma_{\rm min}(\psi)\coloneqq\inf\nolimits_{\theta\in\Psi,\|\theta\|=1}\theta^{\top}\nabla f(\psi)\theta
and
\sigma_{\rm max}(\psi)\coloneqq\sup\nolimits_{\theta\in\Psi,\|\theta\|=1}\theta^{\top}\nabla f(\psi)\theta
with respect to the Jacobian matrix
\nabla f(\psi)
. Then for any
\psi_{1},\psi_{2}\in\Psi
, we have that

\displaystyle\inf\nolimits_{\psi\in\Psi}\sigma_{\rm min}(\psi)\;\leq\;(\psi_{1}-\psi_{2})^{\top}\big(f(\psi_{1})-f(\psi_{2})\big)\;\leq\;\sup\nolimits_{\psi\in\Psi}\sigma_{\rm max}(\psi)

Proof of
Lemma

C.8
.

By the mean value theorem, there exists some
a\in(0,1)
such that

\displaystyle(\psi_{1}-\psi_{2})^{\top}\big(f(\psi_{1})-f(\psi_{2})\big)\;=

\displaystyle\;(\psi_{1}-\psi_{2})^{\top}\big(f(1\times\psi_{1}+0\times\psi_{2})-f(0\times\psi_{1}+0\times\psi_{2})\big)

\displaystyle\;=

\displaystyle\;(\psi_{1}-\psi_{2})^{\top}\nabla f(a\psi_{1}+(1-a)\psi_{2})(\psi_{1}-\psi_{2})

\displaystyle\;=

\displaystyle\;\|\psi_{1}-\psi_{2}\|^{2}\,\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{(\psi_1 - \psi_2)^\top}{\| \psi_1 - \psi_2 \|}$}}\,\nabla f(a\psi_{1}+(1-a)\psi_{2})\,\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\psi_1 - \psi_2}{\| \psi_1 - \psi_2 \|}$}}\;.

Plugging in the definition of
\sigma_{\rm max}
gives the desired upper bound and similarly
\sigma_{\rm min}
implies the lower bound.
∎

Appendix D
Proofs for Online CD

D.1
Auxiliary Lemmas for Online CD

We recall the following notations:

\sigma_{\psi}\coloneqq\mathbb{E}\big[\lVert\big(\phi-\mathbb{E}\big[\phi(\cramped{X^{\psi}})\big]\big)(\cramped{X^{\psi}})\rVert^{2}\big]
,
as well as

\sigma_{\star}\coloneqq\sigma_{{\psi}^{\star}}
and
\sigma\coloneqq\sup_{\psi\in\Psi}\sigma_{\psi}
.

We now provide two intermediary lemmas necessary to analyze the impact of variance in the CD gradient.
The strategy is similar in both of them: we change the integration from
p_{{\psi}^{\star}}
to
p_{\psi}

to obtain contraction, at the cost of an additional term scaling with
C_{\chi}\left\|\psi-{\psi}^{\star}\right\|
.
We obtain exact constants that we choose to describe in terms of the smoothness parameters of the problem, e.g.
the
k^{th}
derivatives of the log partition function
\log Z
, which, for
k\geq 2
, equals
the
k^{th}
derivative of the negative cross-entropy model w.r.t
p_{{\psi}^{\star}}
.

Second Moment convergence

The following lemmas guarantee the second moment of a sample from
(K^{m}_{\psi})_{\#}p_{{\psi}^{\star}}

approaches the second moment of a sample from the target distribution
p_{\psi}
.

Lemma D.1
.

Under
A1
,
A2
and
A3
,
for all
\psi\in\Psi
, we have:

\displaystyle\left\lvert\mathbb{E}[\mathrm{P}_{\psi}^{m}\|\phi(X_{1})\|^{2}]-\mathbb{E}[\|\phi(\cramped{X^{\psi}})\|^{2}]\right\rvert

\displaystyle\leq\alpha^{m}C_{\chi}\left\lVert\psi-{\psi}^{\star}\right\rVert\lVert\log Z\rVert_{1,\infty}\,,

where

\left\lVert\log Z\right\rVert_{1,\infty}\coloneqq\sup_{\psi\in\Psi}\sum_{i=1}^{p}\left(4\partial^{1}_{i}\log Z(\psi)^{2}\partial^{2}_{i}\log Z(\psi)+2\partial^{2}_{i}\log Z(\psi)^{2}\right.\\
\left.\,+\,4\partial^{1}_{i}\log Z(\psi)\partial^{3}_{i}\log Z(\psi)+\partial^{4}_{i}\log Z(\psi)\right)^{1/2}<+\infty\,.

(15)

Proof.

Applying Lemma
C.7
to each
f_{i}\coloneqq\phi_{i}^{2}
, we have

\displaystyle\left\lvert\mathbb{E}\big[\mathrm{P}_{\psi}^{m}\phi_{i}(X_{1})^{2}\big]-\mathbb{E}\big[\phi_{i}(\cramped{X^{\psi}})^{2}\big]\right\rvert

\displaystyle\;=\;\alpha^{m}C_{\chi}\left\lVert\psi-{\psi}^{\star}\right\rVert\Big(\mathbb{E}\big[\left(\phi_{i}(\cramped{X^{\psi}})^{2}-\mathbb{E}\big[\phi_{i}(\cramped{X^{\psi}})^{2}\big]\right)^{2}\big]\Big)^{1/2}

\displaystyle\;=\;\alpha^{m}C_{\chi}\left\lVert\psi-{\psi}^{\star}\right\rVert\Big(\mathbb{E}\big[\phi_{i}(\cramped{X^{\psi}})^{4}\big]-\big(\mathbb{E}\big[\phi_{i}^{2}(\cramped{X^{\psi}})\big]\big)^{2}\Big)^{1/2}

We map the two moments to derivatives of
\log Z(\psi)
, since the
k^{th}
derivative of
\log Z(\psi)
is the
k^{th}
cumulant.
It can be shown, using the multivariate moment to cumulant mapping, that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\phi_{i}(\cramped{X^{\psi}})^{4}\right]

\displaystyle\;=\;\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\partial\log Z}{\partial\psi_i}$}}^{4}+6\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\partial\log Z}{\partial\psi_i}$}}^{2}\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\partial^2 \log Z}{\partial\psi_i^2}$}}+3\left(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\partial^2 \log Z}{\partial\psi_i^2}$}}\right)^{2}+4\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\partial\log Z}{\partial\psi_i}$}}\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\partial^3 \log Z}{\partial\psi_i^3}$}}+\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\partial^4 \log Z}{\partial\psi_i^4}$}}

\displaystyle\;=\;\partial^{1}_{i}\log Z(\psi)^{4}+6\partial^{1}_{i}\log Z(\psi)^{2}\partial^{2}_{i}\log Z(\psi)+3\partial^{2}_{i}\log Z(\psi)^{2}+4\partial^{1}_{i}\log Z(\psi)\partial^{3}_{i}\log Z(\psi)

\displaystyle\quad+\partial^{4}_{i}\log Z(\psi)

where
\partial^{k}_{i}\log Z(\psi)
denotes the
k^{th}
derivative of
\log Z
with respect to
\psi_{i}
.
On the other hand,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\phi_{i}(\cramped{X^{\psi}})^{2}\right]

\displaystyle=\partial^{1}_{i}\log Z(\psi)^{2}+\partial^{2}_{i}\log Z(\psi)

\displaystyle\implies\left(\mathbb{E}\left[\phi_{i}(\cramped{X^{\psi}})^{2}\right]\right)^{2}

\displaystyle=\partial^{1}_{i}\log Z(\psi)^{4}+2\partial^{1}_{i}\log Z(\psi)^{2}\partial^{2}_{i}\log Z(\psi)+\partial^{2}_{i}\log Z(\psi)^{2}

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\phi_{i}(\cramped{X^{\psi}})^{4}]-\left(\mathbb{E}\phi_{i}(\cramped{X^{\psi}})^{2}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle=4\partial^{1}_{i}\log Z(\psi)^{2}\partial^{2}_{i}\log Z(\psi)+2\partial^{2}_{i}\log Z(\psi)^{2}+4\partial^{1}_{i}\log Z(\psi)\partial^{3}_{i}\log Z(\psi)

\displaystyle\qquad+\partial^{4}_{i}\log Z(\psi)

The result follows by summing over
i
, since:

\displaystyle\left\lvert\mathbb{E}\left[\mathrm{P}_{\psi}^{m}\left\lVert\phi(X_{1})\right\rVert^{2}\right]-\mathbb{E}\left[\|\phi(\cramped{X^{\psi}})\|^{2}\right]\right\rvert\;\leq\;\msum\limits_{i=1}^{d}\,\left\lvert\mathbb{E}\left[\mathrm{P}_{\psi}^{m}\phi_{i}(X_{1})^{2}\right]-\mathbb{E}\left[\phi_{i}(\cramped{X^{\psi}})^{2}\right]\right\rvert

Note that
\lVert\log Z\rVert_{1,\infty}
is finite since
\Psi
is compact and
\log Z
is analytic.
∎

Squared First Moment convergence

The next lemma provides convergence (in squared absolute value) of the first moment of the

m
-iterated Markov kernel
k^{m}_{\psi}
.

Lemma D.2
.

Under
A1
,
A2
and
A3
,
for all
\psi\in\Psi
, we have

\left\lvert\mathbb{E}\left[\left\lVert\mathrm{P}_{\psi}^{m}\phi(X_{1})\right\rVert^{2}\right]-{\mathbb{E}\big[\phi(\cramped{X^{\psi}})\big]}^{2}\right\rvert\leq\alpha^{2m}\sigma_{\psi}^{2}+C_{\chi}\alpha^{m/2}\left\lVert\log Z\right\rVert_{2,\infty}\left\lVert\psi-{\psi}^{\star}\right\rVert

where

\left\lVert\log Z\right\rVert_{2,\infty}\;\coloneqq\;\sup_{\psi\in\Psi}\msum\limits_{i=1}^{p}\left(F(\psi)\partial^{2}_{i}\log Z(\psi)\right)^{1/4}+2\left\lvert\partial^{1}_{i}\log Z(\psi)\partial^{2}_{i}\log Z(\psi)\right\rvert^{1/2}

and

F(\psi)\;\coloneqq\;15\partial^{2}_{i}\log Z(\psi)^{3}+10\partial^{3}_{i}\log Z(\psi)^{2}+15\partial^{2}_{i}\log Z(\psi)\partial^{4}_{i}\log Z(\psi)+\partial^{6}_{i}\log Z(\psi)\,.

Proof.

We have:

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[(\mathrm{P}_{\psi}^{m}\phi_{i}(X_{1}))^{2}\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\mathrm{P}_{\psi}^{m}\phi_{i}(X_{1})-\mathbb{E}\left[\phi_{i}(\cramped{X^{\psi}})\right]+\mathbb{E}\left[\phi_{i}(\cramped{X^{\psi}})\right]\right)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\mathrm{P}_{\psi}^{m}\phi_{i}(X_{1})-\mathbb{E}\left[\phi_{i}(\cramped{X^{\psi}})\right]\right)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\quad+2\times\mathbb{E}\left[\phi_{i}(\cramped{X^{\psi}})\right]\,\mathbb{E}\left[\mathrm{P}_{\psi}^{m}\left(\phi_{i}-\mathbb{E}\left[\phi_{i}(\cramped{X^{\psi}})\right]\right)(X_{1})\right]+\left(\mathbb{E}\left[\phi_{i}(\cramped{X^{\psi}})\right]\right)^{2}

Implying

\displaystyle\big\lvert\mathbb{E}\big[\big(\mathrm{P}_{\psi}^{m}\phi_{i}(X_{1})\big)^{2}\big]-\big(\mathbb{E}\big[\phi_{i}(\cramped{X^{\psi}})\big]\big)^{2}\big\rvert

\displaystyle\leq\underbrace{\left\lvert\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\mathrm{P}_{\psi}^{m}\left(\phi_{i}-\mathbb{E}\left[\phi_{i}(\cramped{X^{\psi}})\right]\right)(X_{1})\right)^{2}\right]\right\rvert}\limits_{\Delta_{1}}

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad+2\underbrace{\left\lvert\mathbb{E}\left[\phi_{i}(\cramped{X^{\psi}})\right]\,\mathbb{E}\left[\mathrm{P}_{\psi}^{m}\left(\phi_{i}-\mathbb{E}\left[\phi_{i}(\cramped{X^{\psi}})\right]\right)(X_{1})\right]\right\rvert}\limits_{\Delta_{2}}

where

\displaystyle\Delta_{1}

\displaystyle=\underbrace{\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\mathrm{P}_{\psi}^{m}(\phi_{i}-\mathbb{E}\left[\phi_{i}(\cramped{X^{\psi}})\right])(\cramped{X^{\psi}})\right)^{2}\right]}\limits_{\Delta_{1,1}}

\displaystyle\quad+\underbrace{\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\left(\mathrm{P}_{\psi}^{m}\phi_{i}-\mathbb{E}\left[\phi_{i}(\cramped{X^{\psi}})\right]\right)^{2}(\cramped{X^{\psi}})\left(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\mathrm d p_{\psi^\star}}{\mathrm d p_\psi}$}}(\cramped{X^{\psi}})-1\right)\right)\right]}\limits_{\Delta_{1,2}}

\displaystyle\hskip-10.00002pt\overset{(a)}{\leq}\alpha^{2m}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\phi_{i}-\mathbb{E}\left[\phi_{i}(\cramped{X^{\psi}})\right]\right)(\cramped{X^{\psi}})^{2}\right]+C_{\chi}\left\lVert\psi-{\psi}^{\star}\right\rVert\left(\mathbb{E}\left[(\mathrm{P}_{\psi}^{m}(\phi_{i}-\mathbb{E}\left[\phi_{i}(\cramped{X^{\psi}})\right]))(\cramped{X^{\psi}})^{4}\right]\right)^{1/2}

\displaystyle\hskip-10.00002pt\overset{(b)}{\leq}\alpha^{2m}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\phi_{i}-\mathbb{E}\left[\phi_{i}(\cramped{X^{\psi}})\right]\right)(\cramped{X^{\psi}})^{2}\right]+C_{\chi}\left\lVert\psi-{\psi}^{\star}\right\rVert

\displaystyle\,\,\times\left(\mathbb{E}\left[(\mathrm{P}_{\psi}^{m}(\phi_{i}-\mathbb{E}\left[\phi_{i}(\cramped{X^{\psi}})\right]))(\cramped{X^{\psi}})^{6}\right]\right)^{1/4}\left(\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\mathrm{P}_{\psi}^{m}(\phi_{i}-\mathbb{E}\left[\phi_{i}(\cramped{X^{\psi}})\right])\right)(\cramped{X^{\psi}})^{2}\right]\right)^{1/4}

\displaystyle\hskip-10.00002pt\overset{(c)}{\leq}\alpha^{2m}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\phi_{i}-\mathbb{E}\left[\phi_{i}(\cramped{X^{\psi}})\right]\right)(\cramped{X^{\psi}})^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\,+\alpha^{\frac{m}{2}}C_{\chi}\left\lVert\psi-{\psi}^{\star}\right\rVert\left(\mathbb{E}\left[(\phi_{i}-\mathbb{E}\left[\phi_{i}(\cramped{X^{\psi}})\right])(\cramped{X^{\psi}})^{6}\right]\right)^{1/4}\left(\mathbb{E}\left[(\phi_{i}-\mathbb{E}\left[\phi_{i}(\cramped{X^{\psi}})\right])(\cramped{X^{\psi}})^{2}\right]\right)^{1/4}

In
(a)
, we used the restricted spectral gap Assumption
A3
for
\Delta_{1,1}
, and the Cauchy-Schwarz inequality combined with Assumption
A2
for
\Delta_{1,2}
. In
(b)
, we used Cauchy-Schwarz once again, and in
(c)
we used the fact that
\mathrm{P}_{\psi}^{m}
is a contraction in
L^{6}(p_{\psi})
and another invocation of the spectral gap assumption
A3
.
For
\Delta_{2}
, applying Lemma
C.7
to
f\coloneqq\phi
, we have

\displaystyle\Delta_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}\left[\phi_{i}(\cramped{X^{\psi}})\right]\alpha^{m}\,C_{\chi}\left\lVert\psi-{\psi}^{\star}\right\rVert\left(\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\phi_{i}-\mathbb{E}\left[\phi_{i}(\cramped{X^{\psi}})\right]\right)(\cramped{X^{\psi}})^{2}\right]\right)^{1/2}

\displaystyle\leq\alpha^{m}C_{\chi}\left\lVert\psi-{\psi}^{\star}\right\rVert\partial^{1}_{i}\log Z(\psi)\partial^{2}_{i}\log Z(\psi)^{1/2}

Putting everything together, we have:

\displaystyle\left\lvert\mathbb{E}\left[\mathrm{P}_{\psi}^{m}\phi_{i}^{2}(X_{1})\right]-\mathbb{E}\left[\phi_{i}^{2}(\cramped{X^{\psi}})\right]\right\rvert\;\leq

\displaystyle\;\alpha^{2m}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\phi_{i}-\mathbb{E}\left[\phi_{i}(\cramped{X^{\psi}})\right]\right)(\cramped{X^{\psi}})^{2}\right]+C_{\chi}\left\lVert\psi-{\psi}^{\star}\right\rVert\times

\displaystyle\hskip-100.00015pt\Big(\alpha^{\frac{m}{2}}\left(\mathbb{E}\left[(\phi_{i}-\mathbb{E}\left[\phi_{i}(\cramped{X^{\psi}})\right])(\cramped{X^{\psi}})^{6}\right]\right)^{1/4}\left(\mathbb{E}\left[(\phi_{i}-\mathbb{E}\left[\phi_{i}(\cramped{X^{\psi}})\right])(\cramped{X^{\psi}})^{2}\right]\right)^{1/4}

\displaystyle\hskip-30.00005pt+2\alpha^{m}\partial^{1}_{i}\log Z(\psi)\partial^{2}_{i}\log Z(\psi)^{1/2}\Big)

\displaystyle\;\leq

\displaystyle\;\alpha^{2m}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\phi_{i}-\mathbb{E}\left[\phi_{i}(\cramped{X^{\psi}})\right]\right)(\cramped{X^{\psi}})^{2}\right]+C_{\chi}\alpha^{m/2}\left\lVert\psi-{\psi}^{\star}\right\rVert

\displaystyle\hskip-100.00015pt\Big(\left(\mathbb{E}\left[(\phi_{i}-\mathbb{E}\left[\phi_{i}(\cramped{X^{\psi}})\right])(\cramped{X^{\psi}})^{6}\right]\right)^{1/4}\left(\mathbb{E}\left[(\phi_{i}-\mathbb{E}\left[\phi_{i}(\cramped{X^{\psi}})\right])(\cramped{X^{\psi}})^{2}\right]\right)^{1/4}

\displaystyle+2\left\lvert\partial^{1}_{i}\log Z(\psi)\partial^{2}_{i}\log Z(\psi)^{1/2}\right\rvert\Big)

Summing over
i
, we obtain

\displaystyle\left\lvert\mathbb{E}\left[\|\mathrm{P}_{\psi}^{m}\phi(X_{1})\|^{2}\right]-\left(\mathbb{E}\left[\phi(\cramped{X^{\psi}})\right]\right)^{2}\right\rvert

\displaystyle\leq\sum\limits_{i=1}^{p}\,\left\lvert\mathbb{E}\left[(\mathrm{P}_{\psi}^{m}\phi_{i}(X_{1}))^{2}\right]-\left(\mathbb{E}\left[\phi_{i}(\cramped{X^{\psi}})\right]\right)^{2}\right\rvert

\displaystyle\leq\alpha^{2m}\msum\limits_{i=1}^{p}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\phi_{i}-\mathbb{E}\left[\phi_{i}(\cramped{X^{\psi}})\right]\right)(\cramped{X^{\psi}})^{2}\right]+C_{\chi}\alpha^{m/2}\left\lVert\log Z\right\rVert_{2,\infty}\left\lVert\psi-{\psi}^{\star}\right\rVert

\displaystyle\leq\alpha^{2m}\sigma_{\psi}^{2}+C_{\chi}\alpha^{m/2}\left\lVert\log Z\right\rVert_{2,\infty}\left\lVert\psi-{\psi}^{\star}\right\rVert

where

\displaystyle\left\lVert\log Z\right\rVert_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle=\sup\nolimits_{\psi\in\Psi}\msum\limits_{i=1}^{p}\left(\mathbb{E}\left[(\phi_{i}-\mathbb{E}\left[\phi_{i}(\cramped{X^{\psi}})\right])(\cramped{X^{\psi}})^{6}\right]\right)^{1/4}\left(\mathbb{E}\left[(\phi_{i}-\mathbb{E}\left[\phi_{i}(\cramped{X^{\psi}})\right])(\cramped{X^{\psi}})^{2}\right]\right)^{1/4}

\displaystyle\quad+2\left\lvert\partial^{1}_{i}\log Z(\psi)\partial^{2}_{i}\log Z(\psi)^{1/2}\right\rvert

Similarly to the previous lemma, one can upper bound
\mathbb{E}\left[\big(\phi_{i}-\mathbb{E}\left[\phi_{i}(\cramped{X^{\psi}})\right]\big)(\cramped{X^{\psi}})^{6}\right]
using the
centered
moment to cumulant formula:

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\big(\phi_{i}-\mathbb{E}\left[\phi_{i}(\cramped{X^{\psi}})\right]\big)(\cramped{X^{\psi}})^{6}\right]

\displaystyle=\!15\partial^{2}_{i}\log Z(\psi)^{3}+10\partial^{3}_{i}\log Z(\psi)^{2}+15\partial^{2}_{i}\log Z(\psi)\partial^{4}_{i}\log Z(\psi)+\partial^{6}_{i}\log Z(\psi)

\displaystyle\eqqcolon F(\psi)

To get a full description of
\left\lVert\log Z\right\rVert_{2,\infty}
:

\displaystyle\left\lVert\log Z\right\rVert_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle=\sup\nolimits_{\psi\in\Psi}{\msum\limits_{i=1}^{p}\left(F(\psi)\partial^{2}_{i}\log Z(\psi)\right)^{1/4}+2\left\lvert\partial^{1}_{i}\log Z(\psi)\partial^{2}_{i}\log Z(\psi)^{1/2}\right\rvert}\;.

∎

We can now use the previous lemmas to obtain an expression on the second moment of the contrastive divergence gradient estimator, relating it
to the one of the stochastic log-likelihood gradient estimator.

Lemma D.3
.

Under
A1
,
A2
and
A3
,
we have:

\quad\mathbb{E}\left[\left\lVert h_{t}(\psi,X_{t})\right\rVert^{2}\right]\!\leq 2\sigma_{\star}^{2}+2\sigma^{2}_{\psi}+2L^{2}\left\|\psi-{\psi}^{\star}\right\|^{2}+4\big(\sigma^{2}_{\psi}\alpha^{2m}\!+\!\alpha^{m/2}\left\|\log Z\right\|_{3,\infty}C_{\chi}\left\|\psi-{\psi}^{\star}\right\|\big)

where

\left\lVert\log Z\right\rVert_{3,\infty}\coloneqq 2\max\left(\left\lVert\log Z\right\rVert\vphantom{a}_{1,\infty},\left\lVert\log Z\right\rVert\vphantom{a}_{2,\infty}\right)
.

Proof.

We rely on the following decomposition:

\displaystyle h_{t}(\psi,X_{t})\;=

\displaystyle\;\underbrace{(\phi(X_{t})-\mathbb{E}\left[\phi(X_{1})\right])}\limits_{\Delta_{1,1}}+\underbrace{(\mathbb{E}\left[\phi(X_{1})\right]-\mathbb{E}\big[\phi(\cramped{X^{\psi}})\big])}\limits_{\Delta_{1,2}}

\displaystyle\;+\underbrace{(\mathrm{P}_{\psi}^{m}\phi(X_{t})-\phi(K^{m}_{\psi}(X_{t})))}\limits_{\Delta_{2}}+\underbrace{\left(\mathbb{E}\left[\phi(\cramped{X^{\psi}})\right]-\mathrm{P}_{\psi}^{m}\phi(X_{t})\right)}\limits_{\Delta_{3}}

\Delta_{1,1}+\Delta_{1,2}
form the differentiable stochastic gradient
g_{t}
of Equation
6
.
Note that
\Delta_{1,1}
is mean-zero, and
\Delta_{2}
is mean-zero conditionally on
X_{t}
.
Consequently,

\mathbb{E}\left[\left\langle\Delta_{2},\Delta_{3}\right\rangle\right]=\mathbb{E}\left[\left\langle\Delta_{2},\Delta_{1,1}\right\rangle\right]=\mathbb{E}\left[\left\langle\Delta_{1,1},\Delta_{1,2}\right\rangle\right]=\mathbb{E}\left[\left\langle\Delta_{1,1},\Delta_{2}\right\rangle\right]=0
,
and the only mixed-terms that remain to be controlled are

\mathbb{E}\left[\left\langle\Delta_{1,1},\Delta_{3}\right\rangle\right]
and
\mathbb{E}\left[\left\langle\Delta_{1,2},\Delta_{3}\right\rangle\right]
.
We first control the unmixed terms, and the simple ones first: we have

\mathbb{E}\left[\left\lVert\Delta_{1,1}\right\rVert^{2}\right]=\sigma_{\star}^{2}
, as well as
\mathbb{E}\left[\left\lVert\Delta_{1,2}\right\rVert^{2}\right]\leq L^{2}\left\lVert\psi-{\psi}^{\star}\right\rVert^{2}
.
For
\Delta_{2}
, we have:

\mathbb{E}[\left\lVert\Delta_{2}\right\rVert^{2}\nonscript\>|\nonscript\>\mathopen{}X_{t}]=\mathrm{P}_{\psi}^{m}\left\lVert\phi(X_{t})\right\rVert^{2}-\left\lVert\mathrm{P}_{\psi}^{m}\phi(X_{t})\right\rVert^{2}

We can invoke Lemmas
D.2
and
D.1
, which guarantee

\displaystyle\left\lvert\mathbb{E}\left[\left\lVert\mathrm{P}_{\psi}^{m}\phi(X_{1})\right\rVert^{2}\right]-\left\lVert\mathbb{E}\big[\phi(\cramped{X^{\psi}})\big]\right\rVert^{2}\right\rvert

\displaystyle\,\leq\,\alpha^{2m}\sigma_{\psi}^{2}+C_{\chi}\alpha^{m/2}\left\lVert\log Z\right\rVert_{2,\infty}\left\lVert\psi-{\psi}^{\star}\right\rVert

\displaystyle\left\lvert\mathbb{E}\left[\mathrm{P}_{\psi}^{m}\left\lVert\phi(X_{1})\right\rVert^{2}\right]-\mathbb{E}\left[\left\lVert\phi(\cramped{X^{\psi}})\right\rVert^{2}\right]\right\rvert

\displaystyle\,\leq\,\alpha^{m}C_{\chi}\left\lVert\log Z\right\rVert_{1,\infty}\left\lVert\psi-{\psi}^{\star}\right\rVert

to obtain

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left\lVert\Delta_{2}\right\rVert\vphantom{a}^{2}\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[\left\lVert\phi(\cramped{X^{\psi}})\right\rVert\vphantom{a}^{2}\right]-\left\lVert\mathbb{E}\left[\phi(\cramped{X^{\psi}})\right]\right\rVert^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+\alpha^{2m}\sigma^{2}_{\psi}+C_{\chi}\alpha^{m/2}\left(\left\lVert\log Z\right\rVert\vphantom{a}_{1,\infty}+\left\lVert\log Z\right\rVert\vphantom{a}_{2,\infty}\right)\left\lVert\psi-{\psi}^{\star}\right\rVert

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[\left\lVert\phi(\cramped{X^{\psi}})\right\rVert\vphantom{a}^{2}\right]-\left\lVert\mathbb{E}\left[\phi(\cramped{X^{\psi}})\right]\right\rVert^{2}+\alpha^{2m}\sigma^{2}_{\psi}

\displaystyle\quad+\alpha^{m/2}\left\lVert\log Z\right\rVert_{3,\infty}C_{\chi}\left\lVert\psi-{\psi}^{\star}\right\rVert

where
\left\lVert\log Z\right\rVert_{3,\infty}\coloneqq 2\max(\left\lVert\log Z\right\rVert\vphantom{a}_{1,\infty},\left\lVert\log Z\right\rVert\vphantom{a}_{2,\infty})
.

For
\Delta_{3}
, notice that
\Delta_{3}
is precisely the term
\Delta_{1}
in Lemma
D.2
, and we can thus bound it by

\mathbb{E}\left[\left\lVert\Delta_{3}\right\rVert\vphantom{a}^{2}\right]\leq\sigma\vphantom{a}^{2}_{\psi}\alpha^{2m}+\alpha^{m/2}\left\lVert\log Z\right\rVert_{2,\infty}C_{\chi}\left\lVert\psi-{\psi}^{\star}\right\rVert

Finally, we simply bound
2\mathbb{E}\left[\left\langle\Delta_{1,1},\Delta_{3}\right\rangle\right]
by
\mathbb{E}\left[\left\lVert\Delta_{1,1}\right\rVert\vphantom{a}^{2}\right]+\mathbb{E}\left[\left\lVert\Delta_{3}\right\rVert\vphantom{a}^{2}\right]
,
and
2\mathbb{E}\left[\left\langle\Delta_{1,2},\Delta_{3}\right\rangle\right]
by
\mathbb{E}\left[\left\lVert\Delta_{1,2}\right\rVert\vphantom{a}^{2}\right]+\mathbb{E}\left[\left\lVert\Delta_{3}\right\rVert\vphantom{a}^{2}\right]
.
Putting everything together, we have:

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left\lVert h_{t}(\psi)\right\rVert\vphantom{a}^{2}\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[\left\lVert\Delta_{1,1}\right\rVert\vphantom{a}^{2}\right]+\mathbb{E}\left[\left\lVert\Delta_{1,2}\right\rVert\vphantom{a}^{2}\right]+\mathbb{E}\left[\left\lVert\Delta_{2}\right\rVert\vphantom{a}^{2}\right]+\mathbb{E}\left[\left\lVert\Delta_{3}\right\rVert\vphantom{a}^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\quad+2\mathbb{E}\left[\left\langle\Delta_{1,1},\Delta_{3}\right\rangle\right]+2\mathbb{E}\left[\left\langle\Delta_{1,2},\Delta_{3}\right\rangle\right]

\displaystyle\leq 2\mathbb{E}\left[\left\lVert\Delta_{1,1}\right\rVert\vphantom{a}^{2}\right]+2\mathbb{E}\left[\left\lVert\Delta_{1,2}\right\rVert\vphantom{a}^{2}\right]+\mathbb{E}\left[\left\lVert\Delta_{2}\right\rVert\vphantom{a}^{2}\right]+3\mathbb{E}\left[\left\lVert\Delta_{3}\right\rVert\vphantom{a}^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq 2\sigma_{\star}^{2}+2L^{2}\left\lVert\psi-{\psi}^{\star}\right\rVert^{2}+\sigma^{2}_{\psi}+4(\sigma^{2}_{\psi}\alpha^{2m}+\alpha^{m/2}\left\lVert\log Z\right\rVert_{3,\infty}C_{\chi}\left\lVert\psi-{\psi}^{\star}\right\rVert)

\displaystyle\leq 2\sigma_{\star}^{2}+2\sigma^{2}_{\psi}+2L^{2}\left\lVert\psi-{\psi}^{\star}\right\rVert^{2}+4(\sigma^{2}_{\psi}\alpha^{2m}+\alpha^{m/2}\left\lVert\log Z\right\rVert_{3,\infty}C_{\chi}\left\lVert\psi-{\psi}^{\star}\right\rVert)\,.

∎

D.2
Proof of the SGD recursion (Lemma
3.1
)

We are now ready to provide an SGD-style recursion for the expected squared distance to the optimum

\delta_{t}\coloneqq\mathbb{E}\left[\left\lVert\psi_{t}-{\psi}^{\star}\right\rVert^{2}\right]

for the iterates
\psi_{t}
produced by Algorithm
1
.
We define the following quantities:

\displaystyle\sigma_{\star}=(\mathbb{E}\left[\|\phi(X_{1})-\mathbb{E}\left[\phi(X_{1})\right]\|^{2}\right])^{1/2}\,,\quad\sigma(\psi_{t})=(\mathbb{E}\left[\|\phi(X^{\psi_{t}})-\mathbb{E}\left[\phi(X^{\psi_{t}})\right]\|^{2}\nonscript\>\middle|\nonscript\>\mathopen{}\mathcal{F}_{t}\right])^{1/2}\,,

and
\sigma_{t}\coloneqq\mathbb{E}\left[\sigma(\psi_{t})\right]
.

Lemma

(Restatement of Lemma
3.1
)
.

Under Assumptions
A1
,
A2
and
A3
, for all
t\geq 1
, we have:

\delta_{t}\leq\left(1-2\eta_{t}\tilde{\mu}_{m,t-1}+2\eta_{t}^{2}L^{2}\right)\delta_{t-1}+\eta_{t}^{2}\tilde{\sigma}_{m,t-1}^{2}+4\alpha^{m/2}\eta_{t}^{2}\left\lVert\log Z\right\rVert_{2,\infty}C_{\chi}\delta_{t-1}^{1/2}\,,

where
\left\lVert\log Z\right\rVert_{3,\infty}
is a
constant,

\tilde{\mu}_{m,t}\coloneqq\mu-\alpha^{m}\sigma_{t}C_{\chi}
, and

\tilde{\sigma}_{m,t}\coloneqq(\sigma_{\star}^{2}+\sigma_{t}^{2}+2\sigma^{2}_{t}\alpha^{2m})^{1/2}
.

Proof.

In this proof, we note
(\mathcal{F}_{t})_{t\geq 0}
, the increasing family of
\sigma
-algebras generated by the random variables

(X_{t})_{t\geq 0}\sim p_{{\psi}^{\star}}
and the Markov chain samples
K^{m}_{\psi_{t-1}}(X_{t})
.
We decompose the integrand of
\delta_{t}
as follows:

\displaystyle\left\lVert\psi_{t}-{\psi}^{\star}\right\rVert^{2}

\displaystyle=\left\lVert\mathrm{Proj}_{\Psi}(\psi_{t-1}-\eta_{t}h_{t}(\psi_{t-1},X_{t}))-{\psi}^{\star}\right\rVert^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\left\lVert\psi_{t-1}-\eta_{t}h_{t}(\psi_{t-1},X_{t})-{\psi}^{\star}\right\rVert^{2}\quad\text{(By Lemma \ref{lemma:contraction})}

\displaystyle=\left\lVert\psi_{t-1}-{\psi}^{\star}\right\rVert^{2}-2\eta_{t}\left\langle h_{t}(\psi_{t-1},X_{t}),\psi_{t-1}-{\psi}^{\star}\right\rangle+\eta_{t}^{2}\left\lVert h_{t}(\psi_{t-1},X_{t})\right\rVert^{2}

The first term is (up to an averaging operation) the previous iterate. The middle term will ensure (provided
m
is large enough) contraction of the expected distance to the optimum.
Finally, the third term can be described by Lemma
D.3
, and essentially behaves like the second moment of a log-likelihood stochastic gradient.
Indeed, noting

\overline{g}(\psi_{t-1})\coloneqq-\mathbb{E}\left[\phi(X_{1})\right]+\mathbb{E}\left[\phi(X^{\psi_{t-1}})\right]\,,

the expectation of
g_{t}
w.r.t
x_{t}
(which is the gradient
of the negative cross-entropy between
p_{\psi}
and
p_{{\psi}^{\star}}
), we have:

\displaystyle\left\langle h_{t}(\psi_{t-1},X_{t}),\psi_{t-1}-{\psi}^{\star}\right\rangle

\displaystyle=\left\langle\overline{g}(\psi_{t-1}),\psi_{t-1}-{\psi}^{\star}\right\rangle

\displaystyle\quad+\underbrace{\left\langle(h_{t}(\psi_{t-1},X_{t})-\overline{g}(\psi_{t-1})),\psi_{t-1}-{\psi}^{\star}\right\rangle}\limits_{\Delta}\,.

Applying Lemma
C.7
, we get that

\displaystyle h_{t}(\psi_{t-1},X_{t})-\overline{g}(\psi_{t-1})

\displaystyle=\phi(K_{\psi_{t-1}}^{m}(X_{t})-\mathbb{E}\big[\phi(X^{\psi_{t-1}})\nonscript\>\big|\nonscript\>\mathopen{}\mathcal{F}_{t-1}\big]

\displaystyle\implies\mathbb{E}\left[h_{t}(\psi_{t-1},X_{t})-\overline{g}(\psi_{t-1})\nonscript\>\middle|\nonscript\>\mathopen{}\mathcal{F}_{t-1}\right]

\displaystyle=\mathrm{P}_{\psi_{t-1}}^{m}\phi-\mathbb{E}\big[\phi(X^{\psi_{t-1}}_{1})\nonscript\>\big|\nonscript\>\mathopen{}\mathcal{F}_{t-1}\big]\;,

meaning

\displaystyle\left\lvert\mathbb{E}\left[\Delta\nonscript\>\middle|\nonscript\>\mathopen{}\mathcal{F}_{t-1}\right]\right\rvert

\displaystyle\leq\left\lVert\mathbb{E}\left[\mathrm{P}_{\psi_{t-1}}^{m}\left(\phi-\mathbb{E}\left[\phi(\cramped{X^{\psi_{t-1}}})\right]\right)(X_{t})\nonscript\>\middle|\nonscript\>\mathopen{}\mathcal{F}_{t-1}\right]\right\rVert\left\lVert\psi_{t-1}-{\psi}^{\star}\right\rVert

\displaystyle\leq\alpha^{m}\sigma(\psi_{t-1})C_{\chi}\left\lVert\psi_{t-1}-{\psi}^{\star}\right\rVert^{2}

On the other hand, by applying
Lemma

C.8
to
\overline{g}
, we have:

\left\langle\overline{g}(\psi_{t-1}),\psi_{t-1}-{\psi}^{\star}\right\rangle=\left\langle\overline{g}(\psi_{t-1})-\overline{g}({\psi}^{\star}),\psi_{t-1}-{\psi}^{\star}\right\rangle\geq\mu\left\lVert\psi_{t-1}-{\psi}^{\star}\right\rVert^{2}

Combining the above results, we obtain:

\mathbb{E}\left[\left\lVert\psi_{t}-{\psi}^{\star}\right\rVert^{2}\nonscript\>\middle|\nonscript\>\mathopen{}\mathcal{F}_{t-1}\right]\leq(1-2\eta_{t}(\mu-\alpha^{m}\sigma(\psi_{t-1})C_{\chi}))\|\psi_{t-1}-{\psi}^{\star}\|^{2}\\
+\eta_{t}^{2}(2\sigma_{\star}^{2}+2\sigma^{2}(\psi_{t-1})+2L^{2}\left\lVert\psi_{t-1}-{\psi}^{\star}\right\rVert^{2}\\
+4(\sigma^{2}(\psi_{t-1})\alpha^{2m}+\alpha^{m/2}\left\lVert\log Z\right\rVert_{3,\infty}C_{\chi}\left\lVert\psi_{t-1}-{\psi}^{\star}\right\rVert))

(16)

And the result follows by integrating over
\mathcal{F}_{t-1}
.
∎

D.3
Proof of Online CD convergence

We now prove
Theorem

3.2
.
The recursion of Lemma
3.1
is almost identifiable, up to a cross-term of second order, with the one of an SGD algorithm as presented in the setting of
[
22
, Theorem 1]
.
To make the identification exact, we use the bound
4\alpha^{m/2}\eta_{t}^{2}\left\lVert\log Z\right\rVert_{3,\infty}C_{\chi}\delta_{t-1}^{1/2}\leq 2\alpha^{m/2}\eta_{t}^{2}\delta_{t}+2\alpha^{m/2}\eta_{t}^{2}\left\|\log Z\right\|_{3,\infty}^{2}C_{\chi}^{2}
, yielding the following recursion:

\delta_{t}\leq(1-2\eta_{t}(\mu-\alpha^{m}\sigma C_{\chi})+2\eta_{t}^{2}({L}^{2}\!+\!\alpha^{m/2}))\delta_{t-1}+(\sigma^{2}(2+2\alpha^{2m})\!+\!\alpha^{m/2}\left\lVert\log Z\right\rVert_{3,\infty}^{2}C_{\chi}^{2})\eta_{t}^{2}

where we used the fact that
\tilde{\sigma}_{m,t}\leq\sigma
. This recursion is of the same form as the one studied in
[
22
, Equation 6, Theorem 1]
given by:

\delta_{t}\leq\left(1-2{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\mu}\gamma_{t}+2{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}L}^{2}\gamma_{t}^{2}\right)\delta_{t-1}+2{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\sigma}^{2}\gamma_{t}^{2}

by identifying:

\displaystyle{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\sigma}^{2}

\displaystyle\leftarrow\sigma^{2}(2+2\alpha^{2m})+\alpha^{m/2}\left\lVert\log Z\right\rVert_{3,\infty}^{2}\eqqcolon\tilde{\sigma}_{m}^{2}

\displaystyle{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}L}^{2}

\displaystyle\leftarrow(L^{2}+\alpha^{m/2})\eqqcolon\tilde{L}_{m}^{2}

\displaystyle{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\mu}

\displaystyle\leftarrow\mu-\alpha^{m}\sigma C_{\chi}\eqqcolon\tilde{\mu}_{m}

\displaystyle\gamma_{t}

\displaystyle\leftarrow\eta_{t}

We can use the same unrolling strategy as theirs
(the only condition required to proceed is that
\tilde{\mu}_{m}<\tilde{L}_{m}
, which automatically holds since
\mu<L
), and we obtain

\delta_{n}\leq\begin{cases}2\exp\left(4\tilde{L}_{m}C^{2}\varphi_{1-2\beta}(n)\right)\exp\left(-\frac{\tilde{\mu}_{m}C}{4}n^{1-\beta}\right)\left(\delta_{0}+\frac{\tilde{\sigma}_{m}^{2}}{\tilde{L}_{m}^{2}}\right)+\frac{4C\tilde{\sigma}_{m}^{2}}{\tilde{\mu}_{m}n^{\beta}},&\text{if }0\leq\beta<1\\
\frac{\exp\left(2\tilde{L}_{m}^{2}C^{2}\right)}{n^{\tilde{\mu}_{m}C}}\left(\delta_{0}+\frac{\tilde{\sigma}_{m}^{2}}{\tilde{L}_{m}^{2}}\right)+2\tilde{\sigma}_{m}^{2}C^{2}\frac{\varphi_{\tilde{\mu}_{m}C/2-1}(n)}{n^{\tilde{\mu}_{m}C/2}},&\text{if }\beta=1.\end{cases}

∎

D.4
Proof of online CD with averaging (Theorem
3.3
)

We first restate the theorem in its complete form.

Theorem

(Contrastive Divergence with Polyak-Ruppert averaging)
.

Let
(\psi_{t})_{t\geq 0}
the sequence of iterates obtained by running the CD algorithm
with a learning rate
\eta_{t}=Ct^{-\beta}
for
\beta\in(\frac{1}{2},1)
.
Define
\bar{\psi}_{n}\coloneqq\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\psi_{i}
. Then, under the same assumptions as
Theorem
3.2
we have, for all
n\geq 1
,

\sqrt{\mathbb{E}\left[\left\lVert\overline{\psi}_{n}-{\psi}^{\star}\right\rVert^{2}\right]}\;\leq\;2\sqrt{\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\mathrm{Tr}(\mathcal I(\psi)^{-1})}{n}$}}}+\mathcal{O}\left(n^{\max\left(-\left(\frac{1}{2}+\frac{\beta}{4}\right),-\beta,\frac{\beta}{2}-1,-\left(\frac{\beta}{2}+m\frac{\lvert\log\alpha\rvert}{\log n}\right)\right)}\right)

Where

\mathcal{I}({\psi}^{\star})\coloneqq\mathrm{Cov}\left[\phi(X_{1})\right]

is the Fisher information matrix of the data distribution.
Additionally, if
m>\frac{(1-\beta)\log n}{2|\log\alpha|}
, we have

\sqrt{\mathbb{E}\left[\left\lVert\overline{\psi}_{n}-{\psi}^{\star}\right\rVert^{2}\right]}\;\leq\;2\sqrt{\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\mathrm{tr}(\mathcal I(\psi)^{-1})}{n}$}}}+o\left(n^{-1/2}\right)
.

Recall that we denote by
h_{n}
the standard online CD gradient defined in Equation
3
:

h_{n}(\psi_{n-1},X_{n})=-\phi(X_{n})+\phi(K^{m}_{\psi_{n-1}}(X_{n})),\quad X_{n}\sim p_{{\psi}^{\star}},\quad\forall n\in\mathbb{N}\setminus\{0\},

as well as

\overline{h}(\psi_{n-1})\coloneqq\mathbb{E}\left[h_{n}(\psi_{n-1},X_{n})\nonscript\>\middle|\nonscript\>\mathopen{}\mathcal{F}_{n-1}\right]=\mathbb{E}\left[\phi(X_{n})\right]+\mathbb{E}\big[\phi(K^{m}_{\psi_{n-1}}(X_{n}))\nonscript\>\big|\nonscript\>\mathopen{}\mathcal{F}_{n-1}\big].

In the following, we will hide the dependence on
X_{n}
of
h_{n}(\psi,X_{n})
by writing
h_{n}(\psi)
.
We start by establishing some intermediate lemmas.

Lemma D.4
.

Under Assumptions
A1
,
A2
,
A3
,
the online CD iterates produced by Algorithm
1
using

\eta_{t}=Ct^{-\beta}
for
\beta\in(\frac{1}{2},1)
verify

\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1}{n}$}}\sqrt{\msum\limits_{i=1}^{n}\left(\mathbb{E}\left[\left\lVert\psi_{i}-{\psi}^{\star}\right\rVert^{2}\right]\right)}=\mathcal{O}\left(n^{-\frac{1}{2}-\frac{\beta}{2}}\right).

Proof.

Let us note
\delta_{n}=\mathbb{E}\left[\left\lVert\psi_{n}-{\psi}^{\star}\right\rVert^{2}\right]
.
Summing the r.h.s of Theorem
3.2
, we have

\displaystyle\msum\limits_{i=1}^{n}\delta_{i}

\displaystyle\leq\msum_{i=1}^{n}\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{4 C \tilde{\sigma}_m^2}{\tilde{\mu}_m i^\beta}$}}+2\left(\delta_{0}+\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\tilde{\sigma}_m^2}{\tilde{L}_m^2}$}}\right)\underbrace{\msum_{i=1}^{n}e^{4\tilde{L}_{m}C^{2}\varphi_{1-2\beta}(i)}e^{-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\tilde{\mu}_m C}{4}$}}n^{1-\beta}}}_{A_{3}}

\displaystyle\implies\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1}{n}$}}\sqrt{\msum\limits_{i=1}^{n}\delta_{i}}

\displaystyle\leq\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1}{n}$}}\sqrt{\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{4 C \tilde{\sigma}^2_m}{\tilde{\mu}_m}$}}\varphi_{1-\beta}(n)}+\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1}{n}$}}\sqrt{\Big(2(\delta_{0}+\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\tilde{\sigma}_m^2}{\tilde{L}_m^2}$}})A_{3}\Big)}=\mathcal{O}\left(n^{-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1}{2}$}}-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\beta}{2}$}}}\right).

where
A_{3}
is finite if
\beta<1
, and
A_{3}=O(n)
otherwise
[
22
]
.
∎

Lemma D.5
.

Under Assumptions
A1
,
A2
,
A3
,
the online CD iterates produced by Algorithm
1
using

\eta_{t}=Ct^{-\beta}
for
\beta\in(\frac{1}{2},1)
verify

\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1}{n}$}}\sqrt{\msum\limits_{i=1}^{n}\left(\mathbb{E}\left[\left\lVert\psi_{i}-{\psi}^{\star}\right\rVert^{2}\right]\right)^{1/2}}=\mathcal{O}(n^{-\frac{1}{2}-\frac{\beta}{4}}).

Proof.

Let us note
\delta_{n}=\mathbb{E}\left[\left\lVert\psi_{n}-{\psi}^{\star}\right\rVert^{2}\right]
.
Applying
\sqrt{x+y}\leq\sqrt{x}+\sqrt{y}
to the r.h.s of Theorem
3.2
, we have

\displaystyle\msum\limits_{i=1}^{n}\delta_{i}^{1/2}

\displaystyle\leq\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{2C^{1 / 2} \tilde{\sigma}_m}{2 \tilde{\mu}_m^{1 / 2}}$}}\msum\limits_{i=1}^{n}i^{-\beta/2}+\sqrt{2\Big(\delta_{0}+\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\tilde{\sigma}_m^2}{\tilde{L}_m^2}$}}\Big)}\underbrace{\msum\limits_{i=1}^{n}e^{2\tilde{L}_{m}^{2}C^{2}\varphi_{1-2\beta}(i)}e^{-\frac{\tilde{\mu}_{m}C}{8}i^{1-\beta}}}\limits_{A_{4}}

\displaystyle\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1}{n}$}}\sqrt{\msum\limits_{i=1}^{n}\delta_{i}^{1/2}}

\displaystyle\leq\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1}{n}$}}\sqrt{\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{2 C^{1 / 2} \tilde{\sigma}_m}{\tilde{\mu}_m^{1 / 2}}$}}\varphi_{1-\beta/2}(n)}+\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1}{n}$}}\left(\sqrt{2\left(\delta_{0}+\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\tilde{\sigma}_m^2}{\tilde{L}_m^2}$}}\right)}A_{4}\right)^{1/2}=\mathcal{O}\left(n^{-\frac{1}{2}-\frac{\beta}{4}}\right).

where
A_{4}
is finite if
\beta<1
, and
A_{4}=O(n)
otherwise
[
22
]
.
∎

Lemma D.6
.

Under Assumptions
A1
,
A2
,
A3
, the online CD iterates produced by Algorithm
1
using

\eta_{t}=Ct^{-\beta}
for
\beta\in(\frac{1}{2},1)
verify

\displaystyle\sqrt{\mathbb{E}\left[\left\lVert\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1}{n}$}}\msum\limits_{i=1}^{n}h_{i}(\psi_{i-1})\right\rVert^{2}\right]}=\mathcal{O}\left(n^{\max\left(\frac{\beta}{2}-1,-\frac{2\beta}{2\beta+1}\right)}\right).

Proof.

The main subtlety consists in taking into account the projection step. Indeed, write
h_{t}(\psi_{t})
as:

\displaystyle\psi_{t}

\displaystyle=\mathrm{Proj}_{\Psi}(\psi_{t-1}-\eta_{t}h_{t}(\psi_{t-1}))

\displaystyle=\psi_{t-1}-\eta_{t}h_{t}(\psi_{t-1})+\underbrace{\left(\mathrm{Proj}_{\Psi}(\psi_{t-1}-\eta_{t}h_{t}(\psi_{t-1}))-(\psi_{t-1}-\eta_{t}h_{t}(\psi_{t-1}))\right)}_{\Delta_{t}}

\displaystyle\implies h_{t}

\displaystyle=\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1}{\eta_t}$}}\left(\psi_{t-1}-\psi_{t}\right)+\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\Delta_t}{\eta_t}$}}

\displaystyle\implies\sqrt{\mathbb{E}\left[\left\lVert\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1}{n}$}}\msum_{i=1}^{n}h_{i}(\psi_{i-1})\right\rVert^{2}\right]}

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{\mathbb{E}\left[\left\lVert\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1}{n}$}}\msum_{i=1}^{n}\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1}{\eta_i}$}}\left(\psi_{i-1}-\psi_{i}\right)\right\rVert^{2}\right]}+\sqrt{\mathbb{E}\left[\left\lVert\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1}{n}$}}\msum_{i=1}^{n}\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\Delta_i}{\eta_i}$}}\right\rVert^{2}\right]}

where the last line used Minkowski’s inequality. We now control the two terms separately.
The first term is well-known in the SGD literature, and can be controlled in a straighforward manner.
The second term characterizes the deviation caused by the projection step, and we control it below.

Controlling the second term

We use a similar strategy as in
[
50
]
, but without requiring the assumption on the boundedness of the gradients.
Let us use the notation
h_{t}\coloneqq h_{t}(\psi_{t-1})
for brevity.
First, we have, using again Minkowski’s inequality, that:

\sqrt{\mathbb{E}\left[\left\lVert\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1}{n}$}}\msum_{i=1}^{n}\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\Delta_i}{\eta_i}$}}\right\rVert^{2}\right]}\leq\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1}{n}$}}\msum_{i=1}^{n}\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1}{\eta_i}$}}\sqrt{\mathbb{E}\left[\left\lVert{\Delta_{i}}\right\rVert^{2}\right]}\;.

Note that
\Delta_{t}
is
0
if
\psi_{t-1}-\eta_{t}h_{t}\in\Psi
, e.g.

\Delta_{t}=\left(\mathrm{Proj}_{\Psi}(\psi_{t-1}-\eta_{t}h_{t})-(\psi_{t-1}-\eta_{t}h_{t})\right)\times\mathbb{I}_{\{\psi_{t-1}-\eta_{t}h_{t}\not\in\Psi\}}

Which implies, using Hölder’s inequality, that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left\lVert\Delta_{t}\right\rVert^{2}\right]

\displaystyle={}

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left\lVert\mathrm{Proj}_{\Psi}(\psi_{t-1}-\eta_{t}h_{t})-(\psi_{t-1}-\eta_{t}h_{t})\right\rVert^{2}\times\mathbb{I}_{\{\psi_{t-1}-\eta_{t}h_{t}\not\in\Psi\}}\right]

\displaystyle\leq{}

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left\lVert\mathrm{Proj}_{\Psi}(\psi_{t-1}-\eta_{t}h_{t})-(\psi_{t-1}-\eta_{t}h_{t})\right\rVert^{2p}\right]^{\frac{1}{p}}\times

\displaystyle{}\hskip 200.0003pt\mathbb{P}\left[\left\{\psi_{t-1}-\eta_{t}h_{t}\not\in\Psi\right\}\right]^{\frac{1}{q}}

For any pair
(p,q)
of Hölder conjugates, whose exact value will be determined later.
We investigate the two terms separately; for the first term, note that

\displaystyle\left\lVert\mathrm{Proj}_{\Psi}(\psi_{t-1}-\eta_{t}h_{t})-(\psi_{t-1}-\eta_{t}h_{t})\right\rVert^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 2\left\lVert\mathrm{Proj}_{\Psi}(\psi_{t-1}-\eta_{t}h_{t})-\psi_{t-1}\right\rVert^{2}+2\left\lVert\eta_{t}h_{t}\right\rVert^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 2\left\lVert\mathrm{Proj}_{\Psi}(\psi_{t-1}-\eta_{t}h_{t})-\mathrm{Proj}_{\Psi}(\psi_{t-1})\right\rVert^{2}+2\eta_{t}^{2}\left\lVert h_{t}\right\rVert^{2}

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{\leq}2\left\lVert\psi_{t-1}-\eta_{t}h_{t}-\psi_{t-1}\right\rVert^{2}+2\eta_{t}^{2}\left\lVert h_{t}\right\rVert^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 4\eta_{t}^{2}\left\lVert h_{t}\right\rVert^{2}

where in
(a)
, we used the fact that projections are
1
-Lipschitz.
Thus, we have:

\displaystyle\left(\mathbb{E}\left[\left\lVert\mathrm{Proj}_{\Psi}(\psi_{t-1}-\eta_{t}h_{t})-(\psi_{t-1}-\eta_{t}h_{t})\right\rVert^{2p}\right]\right)^{1/p}

\displaystyle\leq\left(4^{p}\eta_{t}^{2p}\times\mathbb{E}\left[\left\lVert h_{t}\right\rVert^{2p}\right]\right)^{1/p}

\displaystyle\leq 16\eta_{t}^{2}\times\left(\tau_{2p}^{2p}+2C_{\chi}r_{\Psi}\tau_{4p}^{2p}\right)^{1/p}

where we noted, as in Lemma
D.7
,

\tau_{p}\coloneqq\left(\sup_{\psi\in\Psi}\mathbb{E}\left\|\phi\right\|^{p}\right)^{1/p}<+\infty
,

r_{\Psi}=\sup_{\psi,\psi^{\prime}\in\Psi}\left\|\psi-\psi^{\prime}\right\|
,
and we relied on the fact that

\mathbb{E}\Big[\left\lVert h_{t}\right\rVert^{2p}\nonscript\>\Big|\nonscript\>\mathopen{}\mathcal{F}_{t-1}\Big]\leq 2^{2p-1}\left(\tau_{2p}^{2p}+2C_{\chi}r_{\Psi}\tau_{4p}^{2p}\left\lVert\psi_{t-1}-{\psi}^{\star}\right\rVert\right)\\
\leq 2^{2p-1}\left(\tau_{2p}^{2p}+2C_{\chi}r_{\Psi}\tau_{4p}^{2p}\right)

For the probability term, we have:

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left\lVert\psi_{t}-{\psi}^{\star}\right\rVert\vphantom{a}^{4}\right]

\displaystyle\geq\mathbb{E}\left[\left\lVert(\psi_{t}-{\psi}^{\star})\times\mathbb{I}_{\left\{\psi_{t-1}-\eta_{t}h_{t}\not\in\Psi\right\}}\right\rVert\right]^{4}

\displaystyle\geq\mathrm{d}_{{\psi}^{\star}}^{4}\times\mathbb{P}\left[\left\{\psi_{t-1}-\eta_{t}h_{t}\not\in\Psi\right\}\right]

\displaystyle\implies\mathbb{P}\left[\left\{\psi_{t-1}-\eta_{t}h_{t}(\psi_{t-1})\not\in\Psi\right\}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1}{\mathrm{d}_{{\psi}^{\star}}^{4}}$}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\lVert\psi_{t}-{\psi}^{\star}\right\rVert\vphantom{a}^{4}\right]

where we noted

\mathrm{d}_{{\psi}^{\star}}=\inf_{\psi\in\partial\Psi}\left\lVert\psi-{\psi}^{\star}\right\rVert
,
which is positive by
A1
as
{\psi}^{\star}\in\mathrm{int}(\Psi)
.
Combining the two bounds, we thus obtain:

\displaystyle\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1}{n}$}}\msum_{i=1}^{n}\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1}{\eta_i}$}}\sqrt{\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\Delta_{i}\right\|^{2}\right]}

\displaystyle\leq\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1}{n}$}}\msum_{i=1}^{n}\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1}{\eta_i}$}}\times\left(4\eta_{i}\left(\tau_{2p}^{2p}+2C_{\chi}r_{\Psi}\tau_{4p}^{2p}\right)^{1/2p}\right)\times\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1}{d_{{\psi}^{\star}}^{2 / q}}$}}\left(\mathbb{E}\left[\left\lVert\psi_{i}-{\psi}^{\star}\right\rVert\vphantom{a}^{4}\right]\right)^{\frac{1}{2q}}

\displaystyle=\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{
4 \cdpar*{\tau_{2p}^{2p} + 2 C_{\chi} r_{\Psi}\tau_{4p}^{2p}}^{1/ 2 p}
}{
d_{{\psi}^{\star}}^{2 / q}
}$}}\times\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1}{n}$}}\msum_{i=1}^{n}\left(\mathbb{E}\left[\left\lVert\psi_{i}-{\psi}^{\star}\right\rVert\vphantom{a}^{4}\right]\right)^{1/2q}

Recalling that

\displaystyle\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1}{n}$}}\msum_{k=1}^{n}\left(\mathbb{E}\left[\left\lVert\psi_{k}-{\psi}^{\star}\right\rVert\vphantom{a}^{4}\right]\right)^{1/2q}

\displaystyle\leq\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1}{n}$}}\msum_{k=1}^{n}\left(\left(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{12 C^{3 / 2} \tilde{\tau}_1^{2}}{\tilde{\mu}_m^{1 / 2}}$}}\right)^{1/q}\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1}{k^{3 \beta/ 2q}}$}}+\left(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{26 \tilde{\tau}_1^2 C}{\tilde{\mu}_m}$}}\right)^{1/q}\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1}{k^{\mfrac{\beta}{q}}}$}}\right)

\displaystyle\quad+\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1}{n}$}}\msum_{k=1}^{n}\exp\left(-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1}{q}$}}\times\left(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\tilde{\mu}_m C}{16}$}}k^{1-\beta}+16\tilde{L}_{1}^{2}C^{2}\varphi_{1-2\beta}(k)+24\tilde{L}_{1}^{4}C^{4}\right)\right)

\displaystyle\qquad\times\left(32\tilde{\tau}_{1}^{4}C^{4}+\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{160 \tilde{\tau}_1^4 C^3}{\tilde{\mu}_m}$}}+\mathbb{E}\left[\left\lVert\psi_{0}-{\psi}^{\star}\right\rVert\vphantom{a}^{4}\right]+\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{20 C \tilde{\tau}_1^2}{\tilde{\mu}_m}$}}\delta_{0}\right)^{1/2q}

\displaystyle\leq\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1}{n}$}}\left(\left(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{12 C^{3 / 2} \tilde{\tau}_1^{2}}{\tilde{\mu}_m^{1 / 2}}$}}\right)^{1/q}\times\varphi_{1-\frac{3\beta}{2q}}(n)+\left(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{26 \tilde{\tau}_1^2 C}{\tilde{\mu}_m}$}}\right)^{1/q}\times\varphi_{1-\frac{\beta}{q}}(n)\right)

\displaystyle\quad+\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{A_5}{n}$}}\times\left(32\tilde{\tau}_{1}^{4}C^{4}+\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{160 \tilde{\tau}_1^4 C^3}{\tilde{\mu}_m}$}}+\mathbb{E}\left[\left\lVert\psi_{0}-{\psi}^{\star}\right\rVert\vphantom{a}^{4}\right]+\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{20 C \tilde{\tau}_1^2}{\tilde{\mu}_m}$}}\delta_{0}\right)^{1/2q}

where
\tilde{\tau}_{1}
and
\tilde{L}_{1}
are defined in Lemma
D.7
, and

A_{5}=\msum_{k=1}^{n}\exp\left(-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1}{q}$}}\times\left(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\tilde{\mu}_m C}{16}$}}k^{1-\beta}+16\tilde{L}_{1}^{2}C^{2}\varphi_{1-2\beta}(k)+24\tilde{L}_{1}^{4}C^{4}\right)\right)\;,

which is finite if
\beta<1
, and
O(n)
otherwise.
Consequently, we have that

\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1}{n}$}}\msum_{k=1}^{n}\left(\mathbb{E}\left[\left\lVert\psi_{k}-{\psi}^{\star}\right\rVert^{4}\right]\right)^{1/2q}=\mathcal{O}(n^{-\frac{\beta}{q}})\,.

In particular, taking
(p,q)=(\frac{2\beta+1}{2\beta-1},\frac{1}{2}+\beta)
,
we have that

\mathbb{E}\left[\left\lVert\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1}{n}$}}\msum_{i=1}^{n}\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\Delta_i}{\eta_i}$}}\right\rVert^{2}\right]=\mathcal{O}\left(n^{-\frac{2\beta}{2\beta+1}}\right)=o\left(n^{-1/2}\right).

for all
\beta\in\left(\frac{1}{2},1\right)
.

Controlling the first term

The first term was handled in the case of standard SGD
[
22
, Theorem 3]
,
and the only condition needed to reuse their steps is that
(\psi_{t})_{t\leq n}

satisfies an upper bound of the same form as the one
[
22
, Theorem 1]

derived. This is precisely the nature of our bound of
\psi_{t}
estblished in Theorem
3.2
,
with
\tilde{\mu}_{m},\tilde{\sigma}_{m},\tilde{L}_{m}
. Borrowing on their result, we have:

\displaystyle\sqrt{\mathbb{E}\left[\left\lVert\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1}{n}$}}\msum\limits_{i=1}^{n}\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1}{\eta_i}$}}\left(\psi_{i-1}-\psi_{i}\right)\right\rVert^{2}\right]}

\displaystyle\leq\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{4 \tilde{\sigma}_m \beta}{C^{1 / 2} n \tilde{\mu}_m}$}}\varphi_{\beta/2}(n)+\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{4 \beta}{C n \tilde{\mu}_m^{1 / 2}}$}}\Big(\delta_{0}+\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\tilde{\sigma}_m^2}{\tilde{L}_m^2}$}}\Big)^{1/2}A_{2}

\displaystyle\qquad+\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1}{n \tilde{\mu}_m^{1 / 2}}$}}\Big(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1}{C}$}}+2\tilde{L}_{m}\Big)\delta_{0}^{1/2}+\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{2 \tilde{L}_m}{n \tilde{\mu}_m^{1 / 2}}$}}\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{2 C^{1 / 2} \tilde{\sigma}_m}{\tilde{\mu}_m^{1 / 2}}$}}\varphi_{1-\beta}(n)^{1/2}

\displaystyle\qquad+\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{4 \tilde{L}_m}{n \tilde{\mu}_m^{1 / 2}}$}}\Big(\delta_{0}+\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\tilde{\sigma}_m^2}{\tilde{L}_m^2}$}}\Big)^{1/2}A_{2}^{1/2}

where
\tilde{\mu}_{m},\tilde{\sigma}_{m}
and
\tilde{L}_{m}
are defined in Theorem
3.2
, and

A_{2}\;=\;\msum\limits_{k=1}^{n}e^{\frac{-\tilde{\mu}_{m}C}{16}k^{1-\beta}+16\tilde{L}_{m}^{4}C^{4}\varphi_{1-2\beta}(k)}\qed

Lemma D.7
.

Under Assumptions
A1
,
A2
,
A3
,
the online CD iterates produced by Algorithm
1
using

\eta_{t}=Ct^{-\beta}
for
\beta\in(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1}{2}$}},1)
verify

\frac{1}{n}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{n}\sqrt{\mathbb{E}\left[\left\lVert\psi_{i}-{\psi}^{\star}\right\rVert^{4}\right]}=\mathcal{O}(n^{-\beta}).

Proof.

We proceed as in the proof of
[
22
, Theorem 3]
, first establishing a recurrence for

\mathbb{E}\big[\left\lVert\psi_{n}-{\psi}^{\star}\right\rVert^{4}\big]
, and then unrolling it. We have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left\lVert\psi_{n}-{\psi}^{\star}\right\rVert^{4}\nonscript\>\middle|\nonscript\>\mathopen{}\mathcal{F}_{n-1}\right]\leq

\displaystyle\left\|\psi_{n-1}-{\psi}^{\star}\right\|^{4}+6\eta_{n}^{2}\left\lVert\psi_{n-1}-{\psi}^{\star}\right\rVert^{2}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\lVert h_{n}\left(\psi_{n-1},X_{n}\right)\right\rVert^{2}\nonscript\>\middle|\nonscript\>\mathopen{}\mathcal{F}_{n-1}\right]

\displaystyle+\eta_{n}^{4}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\lVert h_{n}\left(\psi_{n-1},X_{n}\right)\right\rVert^{4}\nonscript\>\middle|\nonscript\>\mathopen{}\mathcal{F}_{n-1}\right]

\displaystyle-4\eta_{n}\left\lVert\psi_{n-1}-{\psi}^{\star}\right\rVert^{2}\left\langle\psi_{n-1}-{\psi}^{\star},\mathbb{E}\left[h_{n}(\psi_{n-1},X_{n})\nonscript\>\middle|\nonscript\>\mathopen{}\mathcal{F}_{n-1}\right]\right\rangle

\displaystyle+4\eta_{n}^{3}\left\lVert\psi_{n-1}-{\psi}^{\star}\right\rVert\mathbb{E}\left[\left\lVert h_{n}\left(\psi_{n-1},X_{n}\right)\right\rVert^{3}\nonscript\>\middle|\nonscript\>\mathopen{}\mathcal{F}_{n-1}\right].

The second and fourth terms will be controlled using results from our previous sections.
For simplicity, we don’t attempt to relate the moments of
\left\lVert h_{n}\right\rVert^{4}

as precisely as before. Instead we use the fact that, for all
\psi\in\Psi
,

\mathbb{E}\left[\left\lVert h_{n}(\psi,X_{n})\right\rVert^{k}\right]\leq 2^{k-1}\left(\mathbb{E}\left[\|\phi(K^{m}_{\psi}(X_{n}))\|^{k}\right]+\mathbb{E}\left[\|\phi(X_{n})\|^{k}\right]\right)\,.

Let us note
\tau=\left(\sup_{\psi\in\Psi}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\lVert\cramped{\phi(\cramped{X^{\psi}})}\right\rVert\vphantom{a}^{8}\right]\right)^{1/8}
.
We have, for
k\leq 4
, and
\psi\in\Psi

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\|\phi(K^{m}_{\psi}(X_{n}))\|^{k}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}\|\phi(\cramped{X^{\psi}})\|^{k}

\displaystyle\quad+C_{\chi}\Big(\mathbb{E}\Big[\Big(\big\lVert\phi(\cramped{X^{\psi}})\big\rVert^{k}-\mathbb{E}\Big[\big\lVert\phi(\cramped{X^{\psi}})\big\rVert^{k}\Big]\Big)^{2}\Big]\Big)^{1/2}\big\lVert\psi-{\psi}^{\star}\big\rVert

\displaystyle\leq\tau^{k}+2C_{\chi}\tau^{k}\left\lVert\psi-{\psi}^{\star}\right\rVert\,.

Here, we used the fact that
\mathrm{P}_{\psi_{n-1}}^{m}
is a contraction, and

(\mathbb{E}\left[\left\lVert\phi(\cramped{X^{\psi}})\right\rVert\vphantom{a}^{k}\right])^{1/k}
is an increasing function of
k
for all
\psi
.
On the other hand, we simply have

\mathbb{E}\left[\left\lVert\phi(X_{n})\right\rVert^{k}\right]\leq\tau^{k}
.
Plugging this into the previous equation, applying it to
\psi=\psi_{n-1}
, we obtain

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left\lVert\psi_{n}-{\psi}^{\star}\right\rVert^{4}\ \nonscript\>\middle|\nonscript\>\mathopen{}\mathcal{F}_{n-1}\right]\leq

\displaystyle\left\lVert\psi_{n-1}-{\psi}^{\star}\right\rVert^{4}

\displaystyle+6\eta_{n}^{2}\left\lVert\psi_{n-1}-{\psi}^{\star}\right\rVert^{2}\left(4\tau^{2}+4C_{\chi}\tau^{2}\left\lVert\psi_{n-1}-{\psi}^{\star}\right\rVert\right)

\displaystyle+\eta_{n}^{4}(16\tau^{4}+16C_{\chi}\tau^{4}\left\lVert\psi_{n-1}-{\psi}^{\star}\right\rVert)

\displaystyle-4\eta_{n}\left\lVert\psi_{n-1}-{\psi}^{\star}\right\rVert^{2}\left\langle\psi_{n-1}-{\psi}^{\star},\mathbb{E}\left[h_{n}\nonscript\>\middle|\nonscript\>\mathopen{}\mathcal{F}_{n-1}\right]\right\rangle

\displaystyle+4\eta_{n}^{3}\left\lVert\psi_{n-1}-{\psi}^{\star}\right\rVert\left(8\tau^{3}+8C_{\chi}\tau^{3}\left\lVert\psi_{n-1}-{\psi}^{\star}\right\rVert\right)

To simplify the recursion, we use the four following inequalities:

\displaystyle\tau^{2}\eta_{n}^{2}\left\lVert\psi_{n-1}-{\psi}^{\star}\right\rVert^{3}

\displaystyle\leq\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1}{2}$}}\left(\tau^{2}\eta_{n}^{2}(\left\lVert\psi_{n-1}-{\psi}^{\star}\right\rVert^{2}+\tau^{2}\eta_{n}^{2}\left\lVert\psi_{n-1}-{\psi}^{\star}\right\rVert^{4}\right)

\displaystyle\tau^{4}\eta_{n}^{4}\left\lVert\psi_{n-1}-{\psi}^{\star}\right\rVert

\displaystyle\leq\left(\tau^{4}\eta_{n}^{4}+\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1}{4}$}}\tau^{4}\eta_{n}^{4}\left\lVert\psi_{n-1}-{\psi}^{\star}\right\rVert^{4}\right)

\displaystyle\eta_{n}^{3}\tau^{3}\left\lVert\psi_{n-1}-{\psi}^{\star}\right\rVert

\displaystyle\leq\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1}{2}$}}\left(\eta_{n}^{2}\tau^{2}\left\lVert\psi_{n-1}-{\psi}^{\star}\right\rVert^{2}+16\eta_{n}^{4}\tau^{4}\right)

\displaystyle\tau^{3}\eta_{n}^{3}\left\lVert\psi_{n-1}-{\psi}^{\star}\right\rVert^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1}{2}$}}\left(\eta_{n}^{4}\tau^{4}+\left\lVert\psi_{n-1}-{\psi}^{\star}\right\rVert^{2}\eta_{n}^{2}\tau^{2}\right)

Injecting them in our recursion, we obtain:

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left\lVert\psi_{n}-{\psi}^{\star}\right\rVert^{4}\nonscript\>\middle|\nonscript\>\mathopen{}\mathcal{F}_{n-1}\right]\leq

\displaystyle\left\lVert\psi_{n-1}-{\psi}^{\star}\right\rVert^{4}

\displaystyle+12\eta_{n}^{2}\left\lVert\psi_{n-1}-{\psi}^{\star}\right\rVert^{2}\tau^{2}

\displaystyle+12C_{\chi}\tau^{2}\eta_{n}^{2}\left\lVert\psi_{n-1}-{\psi}^{\star}\right\rVert^{2}

\displaystyle+12C_{\chi}\tau^{2}\eta_{n}^{2}\left\lVert\psi_{n-1}-{\psi}^{\star}\right\rVert^{4}

\displaystyle+16\eta_{n}^{4}\tau^{4}

\displaystyle+16C_{\chi}\eta_{n}^{4}\tau^{4}

\displaystyle+4C_{\chi}\eta_{n}^{4}\tau^{4}\left\lVert\psi_{n-1}-{\psi}^{\star}\right\rVert^{4}

\displaystyle-4\eta_{n}\tilde{\mu}_{m}\left\lVert\psi_{n-1}-{\psi}^{\star}\right\rVert^{4}

\displaystyle+16\eta_{n}^{2}\tau^{2}\left\lVert\psi_{n-1}-{\psi}^{\star}\right\rVert^{2}

\displaystyle+16\eta_{n}^{4}\tau^{4}

\displaystyle+16\eta_{n}^{4}C_{\chi}\tau^{4}

\displaystyle+16\eta_{n}^{2}C_{\chi}\tau^{2}\left\lVert\psi_{n-1}-{\psi}^{\star}\right\rVert^{2}\;,

which, after further simplifications, yields

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left\lVert\psi_{n}-{\psi}^{\star}\right\rVert^{4}\nonscript\>\middle|\nonscript\>\mathopen{}\mathcal{F}_{n-1}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\left\lVert\psi_{n-1}-{\psi}^{\star}\right\rVert^{4}(1-4\eta_{n}\tilde{\mu}_{m}+12C_{\chi}\eta_{n}^{2}\tau^{2}+4C_{\chi}\tau^{4}\eta_{n}^{4})

\displaystyle+\eta_{n}^{2}\left\lVert\psi_{n-1}-{\psi}^{\star}\right\rVert^{2}\left(28(1+C_{\chi})\tau^{2}\right)+32\eta_{n}^{4}\left(\tau^{4}(1+C_{\chi})\right)

\displaystyle\leq\left\lVert\psi_{n-1}-{\psi}^{\star}\right\rVert^{4}(1-4\eta_{n}\tilde{\mu}_{m}+12(1+C_{\chi})\eta_{n}^{2}\tau^{2}+4(1+C_{\chi})\tau^{4}\eta_{n}^{4})

\displaystyle+28\eta_{n}^{2}\left\lVert\psi_{n-1}-{\psi}^{\star}\right\rVert^{2}\left((1+C_{\chi})\tau\right)^{2}+32\eta_{n}^{4}(1+C_{\chi})\tau)^{4}

\displaystyle\leq\left\lVert\psi_{n-1}-{\psi}^{\star}\right\rVert^{4}\left(1-4\eta_{n}\tilde{\mu}_{m}+12\eta_{n}^{2}((1+C_{\chi})\tau)^{2}+4\left((1+C_{\chi})\tau\right)^{4}\eta_{n}^{4}\right)

\displaystyle+28\eta_{n}^{2}\left\lVert\psi_{n-1}-{\psi}^{\star}\right\rVert^{2}\left((1+C_{\chi})\tau\right)^{2}+32\eta_{n}^{4}(1+C_{\chi})\tau)^{4}

\displaystyle\leq\left\lVert\psi_{n-1}-{\psi}^{\star}\right\rVert^{4}(1-4\eta_{n}\tilde{\mu}_{m}+12\eta_{n}^{2}(2(1+C_{\chi})\tau)^{2}+16\eta_{n}^{2}(2(1+C_{\chi})\tau)^{3}

\displaystyle+4(2(1+C_{\chi})\tau)^{4}\eta_{n}^{4})+20\eta_{n}^{2}\left\lVert\psi_{n-1}-{\psi}^{\star}\right\rVert^{2}\left(2(1+C_{\chi})\tau\right)^{2}+16\eta_{n}^{4}(2(1+C_{\chi})\tau)^{4}

\displaystyle\leq\left\lVert\psi_{n-1}-{\psi}^{\star}\right\rVert^{4}(1-4\eta_{n}\tilde{\mu}_{m}+12\eta_{n}^{2}(2(1+C_{\chi})\tau+L)^{2}+16\eta_{n}^{2}(2(1+C_{\chi})\tau+L)^{3}

\displaystyle+4(2(1+C_{\chi})\tau+L)^{4}\eta_{n}^{4})+20\eta_{n}^{2}\left\lVert\psi_{n-1}-{\psi}^{\star}\right\rVert^{2}\left(2(1+C_{\chi})\tau\right)^{2}+16\eta_{n}^{4}(2(1+C_{\chi})\tau)^{4}

\displaystyle\leq\left\lVert\psi_{n-1}-{\psi}^{\star}\right\rVert^{4}(1-4\eta_{n}\tilde{\mu}_{m}+12\eta_{n}^{2}\tilde{L}_{1}^{2}+16\eta_{n}^{2}\tilde{L}_{1}^{3}+4\tilde{L}_{1}^{4}\eta_{n}^{4})

\displaystyle+20\eta_{n}^{2}\left\lVert\psi_{n-1}-{\psi}^{\star}\right\rVert^{2}\tilde{\tau}_{1}^{2}+16\eta_{n}^{4}\tilde{\tau}_{1}^{4}

where we defined

\tilde{\tau}_{1}\coloneqq 2(1+C_{\chi})\tau

and

\tilde{L}_{1}\coloneqq 2(1+C_{\chi})\tau+L
.
This recursion is of the form of the one studied in
[
22
, Equation 32]
(note that by design,
\tilde{L}_{1}\geq\tilde{\mu}_{m}
.)
The steps performed to bound
\mathbb{E}\left[\left\lVert\psi_{n}-{\psi}^{\star}\right\rVert^{4}\right]
thus follow from their derivations, and we obtain:

\displaystyle\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1}{n}$}}\sqrt{\msum\limits_{i=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\lVert\psi_{i-1}-{\psi}^{\star}\right\rVert^{4}\right]}

\displaystyle\;\leq\;

\displaystyle\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{C \tilde{\tau}_1^2}{2 n}$}}\Big(C^{1/2}\varphi_{1-3\beta/2}(n)+\tilde{\mu}_{m}^{-1/2}\varphi_{1-\beta}(n)\Big)

\displaystyle+\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\sqrt{20} C^{1 / 2} \tilde{\tau}_1}{2 n}$}}A_{1}\exp\left(24\tilde{L}_{1}^{4}C^{4}\right)\Big(\delta_{0}+\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\tilde{\mu}_m \cdE*{\cdnorm*{\psi_0 - {\psi}^{\star}}\smallpow{4}}}{20 C \tilde{\tau}_1^2}$}}+2\tilde{\tau}_{1}^{2}C^{3}\tilde{\mu}_{m}+8\tilde{\tau}_{1}^{2}C^{2}\Big)^{1/2}

\displaystyle\;=\;

\displaystyle\mathcal{O}\left(n^{-\beta}\right),

where

A_{1}=\msum\limits_{k=1}^{n}e^{\frac{-\tilde{\mu}_{m}C}{16}k^{1-\beta}+16\tilde{L}_{1}^{4}C^{4}\varphi_{1-2\beta}(k)}

and we have
A_{1}<+\infty
if
\beta<1
, and
A_{1}=O(n)
otherwise.
∎

Lemma D.8
.

For all
\psi\in\Psi
, we have:

\left\lVert\mathrm{Cov}\left[\phi(K^{m}_{\psi}(X_{n}))\right]-\mathrm{Cov}\left[\phi(\cramped{X^{\psi}})\right]\right\rVert_{\mathrm{F}}\\
\leq\alpha^{m}C_{\chi}(\bar{\tau}^{1/2}+2\|\log Z\|_{4,\infty}\sigma)\left\lVert\psi-\psi^{\star}\right\rVert+\alpha^{2m}C_{\chi}^{2}\sigma^{2}\left\lVert\psi-{\psi}^{\star}\right\rVert^{2}.

(17)

where

\bar{\tau}\coloneqq\sup_{\psi\in\Psi}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\lVert\phi(\cramped{X^{\psi}})\phi^{\top}(\cramped{X^{\psi}})-\mathbb{E}[\phi(\cramped{X^{\psi}})\phi^{\top}(\cramped{X^{\psi}})]\right\rVert_{F}^{2}\right]<+\infty

and

\left\lVert\log Z\right\rVert_{4,\infty}\coloneqq\sup_{\psi\in\Psi}\|\mathbb{E}\left[\phi(\cramped{X^{\psi}})\right]\|\leq\sup_{\psi\in\Psi}\msum_{i=1}^{d}\partial_{i}^{2}\log Z(\psi)^{2}
.

Proof.

We have

\mathrm{Cov}\left[\phi(K^{m}_{\psi}(X_{n}))\right]=\mathbb{E}\left[\mathrm{P}_{\psi}^{m}\left(\phi(X_{n})\phi^{\top}(X_{n})\right)\right]-\left(\mathbb{E}\left[\mathrm{P}_{\psi}^{m}\phi(X_{n})\right]\right)\left(\mathbb{E}\left[\mathrm{P}_{\psi}^{m}\phi(X_{n})\right]^{\top}\right)\\

Looking at the second moment first, we have

\mathbb{E}\left[\mathrm{P}_{\psi}^{m}\phi(X_{n})\phi(X_{n})^{\top}\right]-\mathbb{E}\left[\phi(\cramped{X^{\psi}})\phi(\cramped{X^{\psi}})^{\top}\right]=\underbrace{\mathbb{E}\left[\mathrm{P}_{\psi}^{m}(\phi\phi^{\top}-\mathbb{E}\left[\phi(\cramped{X^{\psi}})\phi(\cramped{X^{\psi}})^{\top}\right])(X_{n})\right]}_{\Delta_{1}}.

Applying Lemma
C.7
to the
\mathbb{R}^{d^{2}}
-valued function
f
given by

f_{ij}\coloneqq\phi_{i}\phi_{j}-\mathbb{E}\left[\phi_{i}(\cramped{X^{\psi}})\phi_{j}(\cramped{X^{\psi}})\right]
,

\left\lVert\Delta_{1}\right\rVert_{\mathrm{F}}\leq\left\lVert\psi-{\psi}^{\star}\right\rVert\alpha^{m}C_{\chi}\sqrt{\mathbb{E}\left[\left\lVert\big(\phi\phi^{\top}-\mathbb{E}\left[(\phi\phi^{\top})(\cramped{X^{\psi}})\right]\big)(\cramped{X^{\psi}})\right\rVert^{2}_{F}\right]}\leq\bar{\tau}^{1/2}\alpha^{m}C_{\chi}\left\lVert\psi-{\psi}^{\star}\right\rVert.

We now investigate the first moment. We have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\mathrm{P}_{\psi}^{m}\phi(X_{n})\right]=\underbrace{\mathbb{E}\left[\mathrm{P}_{\psi}^{m}\phi(X_{n})\right]-\mathbb{E}\left[\phi(\cramped{X^{\psi}})\right]}\limits_{\Delta_{2,1}}\;+\;\mathbb{E}\big[\phi(\cramped{X^{\psi}})\big]

\displaystyle\implies

\displaystyle\underbrace{\left(\mathbb{E}\left[\mathrm{P}_{\psi}^{m}\phi(X_{n})\right]\right)\left(\mathbb{E}\left[\mathrm{P}_{\psi}^{m}\phi(X_{n})\right]\right)^{\top}-\mathbb{E}\left[\phi(\cramped{X^{\psi}})\right]\mathbb{E}\left[\phi(\cramped{X^{\psi}})^{\top}\right]}\limits_{\Delta_{2}}=\Delta_{2,1}\Delta_{2,1}^{T}

\displaystyle\quad+\Delta_{2,1}\mathbb{E}\left[\phi(\cramped{X^{\psi}})^{\top}\right]+\mathbb{E}\left[\phi(\cramped{X^{\psi}})\right]\Delta_{2,1}^{\top}\,.

Thus, applying Lemma
C.7
on
\Delta_{2,1}
, we have:

\displaystyle\left\lVert\Delta_{2}\right\rVert_{\mathrm{F}}

\displaystyle\leq\left\lVert\Delta_{2,1}\Delta_{2,1}^{\top}+\Delta_{2,1}\mathbb{E}\left[\phi(\cramped{X^{\psi}})^{\top}\right]+\mathbb{E}\left[\phi(\cramped{X^{\psi}}_{n})\right]\Delta_{2,1}^{\top}\right\rVert_{\mathrm{F}}

\displaystyle\leq\left\lVert\Delta_{2,1}\Delta_{2,1}^{\top}\right\rVert_{\mathrm{F}}+2\left\lVert\Delta_{2,1}\right\rVert\left\lVert\mathbb{E}\left[\phi(\cramped{X^{\psi}})\right]\right\rVert

\displaystyle\leq\alpha^{2m}\sigma^{2}C_{\chi}^{2}\left\lVert\psi-{\psi}^{\star}\right\rVert^{2}+2\left\lVert\log Z\right\rVert_{4,\infty}\alpha^{m}C_{\chi}\sigma\left\lVert\psi-{\psi}^{\star}\right\rVert,

where we used that
\|\Delta_{2,1}\Delta_{2,1}^{\top}\|_{\mathrm{F}}=\|\Delta_{2,1}\|^{2}
.
We can now combine our two matrix moment bounds to obtain

\displaystyle\left\lVert\mathrm{Cov}\left[\phi(K^{m}_{\psi}(X_{n}))\right]-\mathrm{Cov}\big[\phi(\cramped{X^{\psi}})\big]\right\rVert_{\mathrm{F}}=\left\|\Delta_{1}+\Delta_{2}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}

\displaystyle\hskip-250.00038pt\leq\alpha^{m}C_{\chi}(\bar{\tau}^{1/2}+2\|\log Z\|_{4,\infty}\sigma)\|\psi-\psi^{\star}\|+\alpha^{2m}C_{\chi}^{2}\sigma^{2}\left\|\psi-{\psi}^{\star}\right\|^{2}.

∎

Lemma D.9
.

Under Assumptions
A1
,
A2
,
A3
it holds that
